A disabled device slot still had its ADB serial set, so the stream service started a capture for it with no folder, and every retry of that capture killed the captures of all the other devices. A slot with no capture folder is now skipped, and the kill refuses an empty folder. In plain words: one leftover setting can no longer take down every stream on a box · [BUG-0821](../bugs/BUG-0821-2026-10-22-half-disabled-device-slot-kills-every-stream.md)

- **A failing test no longer says the host stopped reporting (BUG-0759)** — When a script ran and failed its check, the task was given the error *"host reported no script result"*. It had reported: `SCRIPT_SUCCESS:false` is the outcome the run exists to produce, and the report, the DB row and every upload were all there. Only the sentence a consumer saw was wrong — but it named an infrastructure fault that did not exist, so every failing test sent people hunting a host problem (it cost this repo a full debugging detour before the two were told apart). The reason is now taken from what the callback actually carried: the host's own stderr if it phrased the failure, otherwise "the test ran and reported a failed result" with the report link, and only a host that genuinely said nothing is described that way. In plain words: a red test now says it is a red test · [BUG-0759](../bugs/BUG-0759-2026-10-21-a-completed-run-was-reported-as-host-reported-no-script-result.md) · area: backend

- **The scope picker shows where the keyboard is (BUG-0799)** — the scope picker is the first tab stop of the app, and focusing it changed nothing on screen: the button base resets the outline and nothing put one back, so a keyboard user could not tell where they were. It now draws a gold outline when the keyboard reaches it, like the rest of the bar · [BUG-0799](../bugs/BUG-0799-2026-10-22-scope-picker-first-tab-stop-has-no-visible-focus-indicator.md)
- **The API workspaces page prints the id that opens the workspace (BUG-0796)** — the card showed the Postman workspace id under the label "Workspace ID", but the app's URL and every backend route resolve the internal id, so copying what was printed opened "Workspace not found". The card now prints the id that works, and shows the Postman id under its own name · [BUG-0796](../bugs/BUG-0796-2026-10-22-api-workspaces-page-prints-an-id-that-does-not-open-the-workspace.md)
- **The *Unknown screens* table headers no longer run into each other on a phone (BUG-0813)** — at 390 px the header row read "ScreenProposed name" and "FramesActions": the columns were percentages of the screen, so the cells were narrower than the words in them, and the text was clipped silently. Every column now gets the width its content needs, the table scrolls sideways inside its card, and a header that does not fit keeps its full text in a tooltip · [BUG-0813](../bugs/BUG-0813-2026-10-22-unknown-screens-table-headers-collide-on-a-phone.md)
- **The unknown-screens thumbnails load again (BUG-0798)** — a capture that arrived inline as a `data:` URI was sent to the signing service like any other path, so the browser asked for a URL whose path was the base64 payload and got nothing: four of the fourteen thumbnails on *Unknown screens* were empty grey boxes, with no error. An inline capture is now used as it is, and the server refuses to sign one · [BUG-0798](../bugs/BUG-0798-2026-10-22-unknown-screens-thumbnails-empty-when-capture-is-an-inline-data-uri.md)
- **The unknown-screens page says when the interface does not exist, instead of reporting all clear (BUG-0795)** — `/configuration/interface/<name>/unknown` answered a healthy report for a name that is not an interface: 0 needs a name, 0 filtered out, 0 captures, "Every screen this device shows has a node in its tree". A typo in the address looked like an interface without unknown screens, and hid the real cause. The server now answers 404 for an interface that does not exist, and the page shows that instead of the report · [BUG-0795](../bugs/BUG-0795-2026-10-22-unknown-screens-page-reports-all-clear-for-a-missing-interface.md)
- **The OpenRouter debug tool points at the AI settings that replaced it (BUG-0797)** — `/configuration/openrouter` was still a full page with a model list, a prompt box and an Execute button, but the backend route it called was removed with the AI unification, so every click answered 400 and the page looked like a working tool that is broken. The page is retired and the old address now opens Settings → AI, where an endpoint is tested against a model and the answer is shown · [BUG-0797](../bugs/BUG-0797-2026-10-22-openrouter-debug-page-calls-a-backend-route-that-was-deleted.md)
- **The API reference covers every published spec, and the dashboard contract parses again (BUG-0800)** — the "Select API" dropdown offered 14 of the 19 published specs, so the dashboard, log-review and masterchief routes had no contract a developer would find, and the one file that had it (`server-dashboard.yaml`) was not valid YAML: an unquoted description with a colon in it, which made the reference viewer, Postman and every client generator refuse the whole file. The dropdown is now built from the files in `docs/api/specs/`, so a new spec cannot be forgotten again, and every published spec is parsed by the test suite · [BUG-0800](../bugs/BUG-0800-2026-10-22-api-reference-offers-14-of-19-specs-and-dashboard-spec-is-invalid-yaml.md)
- **The AI Dataset review page shows the screenshot it asks you to judge (BUG-0775)** — every sample on `/training-review` showed a broken-image glyph with no message: the training pictures are served from a host whose nginx answers only three allow-listed IPs, so the browser got an HTML 403 and blocked it. The reviewer answered blind, and the answer went into the reference set. The server now reads the picture out of the training bucket and serves it through the app, so the page works for every user; a picture that cannot be loaded says so and keeps the answer buttons off, because a screen you cannot see is not one you can judge The image route serves only `image/*` objects (415 otherwise), sends `nosniff` and caps the read size · [BUG-0775](../bugs/BUG-0775-2026-10-22-ai-dataset-review-shows-broken-screenshots-with-no-error.md)
- **The navigation editor says when a tree does not exist, instead of opening an empty one (BUG-0774)** — `/navigation-editor/<name>` for a name that is not an interface (or an interface that has no prod snapshot) used to open a blank editor: `0/0 nodes`, a live **Add Node** and **Publish**, and no message at all. A typo or a stale bookmark looked like an empty tree, so a developer could add and publish nodes into the wrong place. The editor now says `User interface "<name>" not found` (or `No prod snapshot published for "<name>" — it exists in dev only`), offers a **Back to Interfaces** button, and shows no toolbar at all · [BUG-0774](../bugs/BUG-0774-2026-10-22-navigation-editor-opens-empty-editor-for-unknown-tree.md)
- **Monitoring > System no longer shows Grafana's login form instead of a dashboard (BUG-0772)** — the page embedded the Grafana dashboard whatever Grafana answered, and Grafana was answering **401**: what an operator got inside the app was Grafana's own "Welcome to Grafana" login form, above a picker full of dashboards that could not open. Grafana keeps its own session and nothing bridges it to the app's, so the page now asks Grafana first and shows what it answered: a sign-in panel with an **"Open Grafana to sign in"** button (a new tab) and a **"Check again"** button — sign in once there, click Check again, and the dashboards open inside the app, because the session cookie is first-party on the app's own origin. The picker now appears only when there is something true to pick, and it lists the dashboards this viewer may open. In plain words: the app tells you what is missing instead of showing you a login page · [BUG-0772](../bugs/BUG-0772-2026-10-22-monitoring-system-shows-grafana-login-form.md) · area: monitoring

- **The Status page shows every host once, and its "services healthy" tile counts each service once (BUG-0771)** — host-clone-1 and host-clone-2 each appeared **twice** in the "VM summary" table, with identical numbers, because the page built one row per *catalog entry* and those two machines are registered on the primary server **and** on the QualiAI server (same host name, same API address). Every service of a duplicated host was therefore added to the health count twice: the tile read 53/57 on the live platform. One physical host is now one row, one section and one set of services, whatever number of servers reports it. The Dashboard > System card now says **"Platform services healthy"** instead of the same words the Status page uses, because it counts the platform's own units (29) and not the hosts — the full picture stays on /status. In plain words: the same machine is no longer listed twice, and the health count counts what it says · [BUG-0771](../bugs/BUG-0771-2026-10-22-status-page-lists-the-same-host-twice.md) · area: monitoring

- **The Interface, Device Models and References tables line up again, and their rows are half as tall (BUG-0768)** — on `/configuration/interface` every column sat under the wrong header (Models 12px out, Version 86px, Variants 172px, Actions 250px, the drift growing to the right) and each row was 60px instead of 36px, so a 58-row list no longer fit on screen. A fix for the spinner-instead-of-skeleton defect had hoisted each page's whole `<Table>` into a const so the loading placeholder would share its geometry, which carried the `<Table>` opening tag with it and left the `<TableBody>` a **sibling** of the table rather than inside it. The page then held two independent tables: the header kept its fixed percentage widths and filled the card, while the body fell back to content-based auto layout and shrink-to-fit, so the two column algorithms could not agree — and because the compact cell padding was a descendant rule of the header's `<Table>`, the body reverted to the theme's default padding. All three pages are fixed by hoisting the `<TableHead>` alone and assembling head and body through a function that takes the body as an argument, so the `<tbody>` is lexically inside the `<table>` that closes it and cannot escape again. Rows are also pinned to one shared height number that the live row and the loading placeholder both read, so a list no longer jumps when its rows arrive. In plain words: the columns are back under their headers and the rows are half the height · [BUG-0768](../bugs/BUG-0768-2026-10-22-interface-tables-body-outside-the-table-columns-drift-rows-double-height.md) · area: frontend

- **One device with a missing model no longer stops monitoring for the whole host** — a device whose monitor profile cannot start at boot (for example `counting_cars` without `yolox_nano.onnx`) was meant to be left out and retried, but the error message used a setting that was not set yet, so `vpt-monitor-img` crashed and restarted every few seconds and no device on the host was analysed (seen on vpt-lenovo1). Now only that device is left out · area: monitoring


- **IRTrans raw IR keys reach the device again (BUG-0767)** — In raw mode (no `IR_REMOTE`) VPT wrote the IR output as `sndccf <code>,l13`, which the IRTrans refuses for every code (`Illegal / not supported CCF Code`), while Take Control still said the key was sent. It now sends `sndccf <code> l13`, which targets the output, and reads the box's answer, so a refused key shows as an error with the box's message. In plain words: Apple TVs and other raw-code devices on an IRTrans box respond, and when the box says no, you see it · patch-2 (patch/patch_host_2_irtrans-any-device.sh) · [BUG-0767](../bugs/BUG-0767-2026-10-22-irtrans-raw-key-refused-led-after-comma.md)

- **Every device tile on one host hung on "Loading stream..." while the host itself looked perfect (BUG-0765)** — On the `vpt-pi4` server, all seven `vpt-lenovo1` tiles stayed on "Loading stream..." (or "Snapshot unavailable" before hover) while the `vpt-nipogi` tiles on the same page played fine. The host was not the problem: ffmpeg ran per device, `output.m3u8` was rewritten every second, segments counted up, the registry said `online`, and fetching the playlist straight from the host's own port returned 200. The server's nginx had never been given a `location /host/vpt-lenovo1/` block — only the noVNC regex block on `:6080` — so every stream and thumbnail request fell through to `location /` and hit the Flask backend, which has no such route: 404, and the tile waited forever. The health checks missed it because they call the host API directly and never go through nginx. The seventh tile, `CNews`, had a second, unrelated fault: its Dailymotion URL was a signed CDN token that expires, the manifest began answering 403, and ffmpeg restart-looped on it. It was re-probed from the host's own French IP and replaced with **BFM2** (CloudFront, no custom User-Agent needed). All seven tiles now play. In plain words: the screens were never broken — the door to them was, and one door had rotted · [BUG-0765](../bugs/BUG-0765-2026-10-22-lenovo1-device-tiles-stuck-loading-no-nginx-block.md) · area: infra
- **The fixnet call test now proves a call is picked up and connected end to end (BUG-0766)** — `--answer true` could never pass: it demanded `CONNECT` in the reply to `ATA`, and on this modem firmware every way of picking up was wrong. Proven on the rack, each the cause of one failed run: `ATA` in data mode returns nothing and leaves the modem deaf; switching to voice mode between the ring and the pickup makes the modem forget the call; `AT+VLS=1` before the call takes the line off-hook so the dial answers `BUSY`; and `ATA` in voice mode answers `ERROR`. The called line is now armed with `AT+FCLASS=8` in its prepare step and picks up with `AT+VLS=1` during the ring. A voice pickup gives the calling modem no `CONNECT` (there is no carrier), so a new step, *Call connected end to end*, proves the call on the line: the called line hears no dial or busy tone while held, the caller has not dropped, and when the caller hangs up the called line hears it. A failed call no longer fails the release as well: the release still runs, but as cleanup that does not decide the verdict. **The pickup is part of every run** — a plain run is nine steps, with `--answer false` kept as the opt-out for a far end that answers on its own. Rack run 2026-10-22: 9/9 pass. **Digits (DTMF) and voice audio stay open** — a green run covers line state only. In plain words: the fixnet test now proves a call is answered and both ends are in it · [BUG-0766](../bugs/BUG-0766-2026-10-22-fixnet-answer-asked-for-connect-on-the-answering-modem.md) · TASK-71 · area: testing
- **A phone on adb screencap streams as soon as it is in the host .env** — the screen-capture loop of a screencap phone (`DEVICEn_ADB_SERIAL` + `DEVICEn_VIDEO=.../emulator_frames/deviceN/latest.png`) used to need its own `systemctl enable vpt-emulator-fifo@deviceN` by hand, so a correctly configured phone showed no stream. The stream service now starts that loop for every such device each time it starts, and stops the loop of a device removed from the .env. In plain words: add the phone to the .env, restart the stream service, and its screen shows up · patch-5 (patch/patch_host_5_screencap-loop.sh) · [BUG-0762](../bugs/BUG-0762-2026-10-22-a-phone-on-adb-screencap-showed-no-stream-because-nothing-started-its-capture-loop.md)
- **The dashboard stopped claiming a healthy host was in error (BUG-0758)** — The red **"1 in error"** badge sat over a host card with nothing wrong on it (Stream and Host API green, the Services LED green, the stopped services folded into a grey "Optional (4)"), and the badge could never point at a cause: every service that tripped it was one the card deliberately hides. The badge asked "is this service optional?" while the Services card asked "does this host need it?", and on a host where TASK-144 stops what no device profile wants they answer differently — the backend marks those services stopped **on purpose** and flips `critical`, not `optional`. The verdict now comes from the same model the card renders, so **the badge can only light up when something red is on screen**. The Auto-fix button stops offering those four "down services" on a healthy host too (following it would have restarted exactly what the profiles switched off). A code mismatch is now an **amber "1 in warning"** chip and an amber version line instead of a red one, because a build behind its server is a deploy to schedule, not a broken host — and a host the backend reports degraded is a warning for the same reason: a service that is missing rather than down never draws a row. In plain words: if the badge is red, something on the card is red · [BUG-0758](../bugs/BUG-0758-2026-10-21-dashboard-error-badge-vs-services-card.md)
- **A report's screenshots now show the run that took them, not the run next to it (BUG-0757)** — Since two web scripts can run side by side on one host (TASK-146), a report's step screenshots could belong to the *other* run: a host has one screen (`DISPLAY=:1`), both Chrome windows draw on it, and the report took its image from the host's screen capture — the newest photo of the whole screen, so whoever's window was on top won. The verdict and the data were always right, but the picture you judge the run by showed somebody else's browser. A run now photographs its own page instead. The screen capture stays the fallback for a device that drives no page (a TV, an Android box) or whose browser died mid-shot — except for the run's before/after shots, where a browser run has no page by definition (before it launches Chrome, after it closes one) and the shared screen would show the other run's window: there, no image beats the wrong one, and the run log says why. In plain words: run two web tests on one host and each report shows its own site · [BUG-0757](../bugs/BUG-0757-2026-10-21-with-two-browser-runs-a-report-screenshot-showed-the-other-runs-browser.md) · TASK-146 · area: testing

- **A Fire TV on IRTrans shows only the IR remote (BUG-0755)** — An `android_tv` (or `fire_tv_fireos`) device always got an Android TV adb remote, even with no `DEVICE<N>_IP` or `DEVICE<N>_ADB_SERIAL`, so a box driven only by IRTrans showed a second remote that reached nothing. The adb remote is now built only when one of them is set. The same models now accept the power API (`DEVICE<N>_POWER_NAME`, `POWER_API_URL`, `POWER_API_KEY`), which was only read for `stb`. In plain words: configure the IR blaster and you get one remote, and power on/off works through the API · [BUG-0755](../bugs/BUG-0755-2026-10-21-android-tv-adb-remote-built-without-ip.md)

- **The Apple TV IR remote sends real Apple codes (BUG-0754)** — `ir_conf/apple_tv.json` was a copy of the Samsung TV keymap, so `IR_TYPE=apple_tv` sent Samsung codes the Apple TV ignored. It now holds the official Apple remote set (NEC, address `0x87EE`): power, arrows, OK, menu/back, play/pause, forward and rewind. HOME, volume, channel and mute have no Apple code and show as not mapped. In plain words: an Apple TV on an IR blaster now answers its remote · [BUG-0754](../bugs/BUG-0754-2026-10-21-apple-tv-ir-keymap-held-samsung-codes.md)

- **Apple TV IR remote buttons sat in the top-left corner on older client builds (BUG-0753)** — On installs built before 2026-10-06 the Apple TV panel showed the Magenta TV remote picture with the clickable boxes bunched in its corner, so clicking a visible button sent nothing. Current builds already have the aligned layout; client patch-4 puts it on an older install and rebuilds the frontend. In plain words: the Apple TV remote buttons are under the picture again · patch-4 (patch/patch_frontend_4_appletv-remote-layout.sh) · [BUG-0753](../bugs/BUG-0753-2026-10-21-apple-tv-remote-buttons-bunched-in-the-corner-on-older-builds.md)

- **Atlas answers within a budget and finds the docs again (BUG-0752)** — a docs question ("which IR standard do you use, share the EOS remote file") ran 220 s and about 40 tool calls without an answer. The master docs index pointed to about 50 agent docs at paths that had moved, so every file it suggested was "not found"; it now has the real paths, and a missing path answers "Did you mean ...". Doc search now finds files that contain every word of the query instead of the exact phrase, and ranks guides above bug reports. Each Atlas answer now has a budget, stated to the model: 20 s of model time and 12 tool calls by default (device and tree-building skills get more), plus the skill's time limit; past it Atlas answers with what it found and says what is missing. Copying a conversation now shows each tool call's own result, not the first result of that tool. In plain words: Atlas stops searching after about 20 seconds and answers, and the IR doc is found · [BUG-0752](../bugs/BUG-0752-2026-10-21-atlas-docs-question-ran-220-s-and-never-found-the-ir-doc.md)
- **A misspelled IR_TYPE now says what is wrong (BUG-0751)** — `DEVICEn_IR_TYPE=appletv` instead of `apple_tv` made Take Control show only "Failed to connect to remote controller". The toast and the host log now name the missing keymap, list the ones that exist and suggest the closest (`Did you mean 'apple_tv'?`), for the IRTrans, IR Toy and lirc remotes. In plain words: a typo in the remote setting tells you the right spelling · [BUG-0751](../bugs/BUG-0751-2026-10-21-misspelled-ir-type-gives-a-generic-remote-error.md)

- **Eight more pages drew the shape of the content they were about to fill instead of a bare spinner (BUG-0750)** — The third sweep for the same defect BUG-0744 / BUG-0747 / BUG-0748 set out to fix found it on eight more pages: **Settings**, **AI Queue Monitor**, **Requirements**, **Users**, **Teams**, **Roles**, **Workspaces** and **API Workspace Detail**. The mobile-app feature pages (`/configuration/mobile-app`, `/mobile-app/this-phone`) and the CI/CD feature page (`/test-execution/cicd`) sat on the same anti-pattern too. Every one of them jumped from "an empty page with a centred spinner" to "the real page chrome with a grey skeleton in the body" — Settings keeps its tabs + Save bar + alerts mounted and skeletonizes the active tab; the four admin tables keep their Card + TableHead + colgroup and put placeholder rows behind the real header; AI Queue Monitor keeps its PageTitle + segmented toggles and draws four bordered tile placeholders + a 12-row skeleton under the existing TableHead; the mobile and CI/CD pages keep their QR / form chrome and only skeletonize the data-dependent body. The bare-spinner defect is now gone from every report-style and admin-style page; small per-cell and per-row spinners (the inline loading indicator, the per-row coverage loader in Requirements, the per-cell Variants spinner in `/configuration/interface`) were left on purpose. In plain words: every page with a body now shows that body's shape while it loads · [BUG-0750](../bugs/BUG-0750-2026-10-21-eight-more-pages-drew-a-spinner-instead-of-their-own-shape.md) · area: frontend
- **A device model spelled slightly differently now builds the same device (BUG-0749)** — `DEVICE4_MODEL` is typed by hand, and `androidtv` instead of `android_tv` used to be fatal rather than cosmetic: the host looked the name up as a bare key, missed, and built the device with **no controllers at all** — so it appeared on the dashboard, streamed fine, and Take Control answered "Unsupported device" with no remote, no keys and no clue which name was wrong. Model names are now resolved to the one they mean wherever they are read — the `.env`, the controller factory, the model routes and the capture script — in three passes: the exact name, the same name without its separators or its case (`androidtv`, `ANDROID_TV`, `Android TV`, `android-mobile`, `appletv`, `firetv_vega`), and a near miss for a genuinely mistyped name (`andoridtv` → `android_tv`, `stbb` → `stb`). Every model already in the table is guaranteed to stay itself, so the tolerance can never make one name shadow another, and a device name carrying a platform tag (`stb-example-v2`) keeps its tag. What is **not** guessed at: a value that could be several models (`android`, `runner`, `ios`), a numbered variant (`rtsp-video-2`) or a device type this project has no controllers for (`samsung_tv`) is still reported as unknown with the real names listed — a device built out of the wrong model is worse than no device, because it looks alive and answers to controls that cannot work. One family name is chosen for you and logged rather than done silently: a bare `fire_tv` reads as the common Fire TV (`fire_tv_fireos`), with a `DEVICE4_MODEL: 'fire_tv' read as 'fire_tv_fireos'` line in the host log, and the Vega box, which has its own model, is spelled `fire_tv_vega`. Add Model on the Device Models page accepts the same spellings and stores them canonically, so `appletv` can no longer become a second row for `apple_tv` that no userinterface would ever match. In plain words: spell the model how you like and it still works · [BUG-0749](../bugs/BUG-0749-2026-10-21-device-model-misspelling-gives-a-device-with-no-controllers.md) · area: device-control

- **Six more pages showed a spinner in an empty area instead of the content they were about to fill (BUG-0748)** — The follow-up sweep for BUG-0744 and BUG-0747 found the same loading half of that defect on six more pages (no wrong numbers this time — just an empty page that fills in a moment later): **Interfaces**, **Device Models**, **Interface → References**, **Alerts**, **Coverage** and **Localize Coverage**. Each replaced the content it was about to show with a centred spinner, and on three of them the spinner threw away the table header too, so the page reserved no column widths and no row count. Alerts was the worst: one spinner row spanned all seven columns. All six now draw the layout they are about to fill, from the same grey skeleton as the report pages — the table header stays put and the rows behind it are placeholders, so nothing moves when the data lands. **Coverage**'s category list is a grid rather than a table, so it draws seven rows on the same grid with a bar in each cell; **Localize Coverage** draws the phone's card list on a phone and the desktop's six-column table on a desktop. A small spinner inside a row, inside a button, or inside a drawer is still the right thing and was left alone. In plain words: every page now shows its own shape while it loads, instead of a spinner floating in white space · [BUG-0748](../bugs/BUG-0748-spinner-instead-of-skeleton-six-more-pages.md) · area: frontend
- **Model Reports showed a red `N/A`, and two report pages hid their table behind a spinner (BUG-0747)** — The same defect BUG-0744 fixed on Test Reports and CI/CD Reports, still sitting on the other two pages of the Report menu. On **Model Analysis Reports** the Success rate tile read `N/A%` in red whenever there were no executions — on the first load and on an empty result set — because the rate was the string `'N/A'`, `Number('N/A')` is `NaN`, and every comparison against `NaN` is false, so the colour chain fell through to its last branch: the error colour. An unknown value was drawn like a failed one. **Model Analysis Reports** and **Dependency Report** also replaced their whole table with a single centred spinner, which reserves nothing, so the page showed a mark in an empty area and then popped the real table into place (Dependency Report did it in four tables). The rate now falls back to a muted dash instead of a red `N/A%`, every number in the tile row is a grey placeholder while the data lands instead of a row of `0`s, and both pages draw their real column widths while loading — same skeleton as the other report pages, so all four look alike. In plain words: the other two report pages stop showing a red `N/A` and start showing the shape of what is coming, like the first two already did · [BUG-0747](../bugs/BUG-0747-model-and-dependency-report-loading-state.md) · area: frontend
- **A screen judged harmless no longer raises freeze alerts again (BUG-0746)** — a device resting on its home screen or a menu (vpt-mf1's Android TV raised 56 freezes in 3 days, each judged "expected" by the AI) is now remembered for every device, not only the host desktop: once the screen is judged expected, the monitor no longer opens a freeze on it at all. A screen is remembered from its first sighting and kept by usage: seen often, it stays for good; seen once or twice and not again for a week, it is dropped as random. A device with no userinterface also gets Localize's default one, which names the screen from that memory ("known screen #id, expected") or says unknown. In plain words: a menu that is meant to be still stops showing up as a freeze · [BUG-0746](../bugs/BUG-0746-2026-10-21-a-discarded-screen-froze-again-and-again.md) · [TASK-84](../tasks/TASK-84-screen-memory-localize-triage.md)

- **Auto-fix now actually fixes a host screen capture that died on "Authorization required" (BUG-0745)** — A device that captures the desktop over X11 (`HOST_VIDEO_SOURCE=:1`) died on startup with `Authorization required, but no authorization protocol specified` / `Cannot open display :1`, and the dashboard's **auto-fix** button could never clear it: auto-fix restarts a service, and restarting cannot fix a fault that is not about the service. The X cookie the capture needs was either missing or no longer the one the X server accepted, so every restart re-ran the same failing launch, and the stall watchdog's own 60-second retry produced the same error on the same schedule — which is why the host looked busy rather than broken, and why the only way out was an SSH session. Both entry points now self-heal. Every x11grab start asks whether the display is actually readable and, if it is not, repairs it before building the ffmpeg command, so the dashboard's restart, a systemd restart, the watchdog and a source switch all heal the same way; and the watchdog recognises the fault signature in the per-device log and repairs **before** restarting, so the restart lands on a working cookie instead of burning a retry. The repair escalates cheapest-first and proves itself: re-resolve the cookie from the X server's own `-auth` path, then grant `xhost +local:` so same-host connections stop depending on cookie matching (this is also the fix for a host whose `xstartup` never ran that grant), then a real one-frame `x11grab` that has to land a file on disk before success is claimed. A healthy display is left completely alone — one probe, no grant, nothing changed — a log that *once* showed the fault while the capture runs fine is not repaired again, an ordinary output stall keeps going to the existing restart/dormant path untouched, and if none of it works the host is told plainly that it fell back rather than claiming a repair. Restarting the VNC server is deliberately left out: it would regenerate the cookie from scratch, but it also drops any live VNC session, so that stays your call. In plain words: press auto-fix on a dead desktop capture and it comes back, instead of the same error every minute · [BUG-0745](../bugs/BUG-0745-2026-10-21-x11grab-x11-auth-autofix.md) · 🩹 patch-1 (`patch/patch_host_1_x11-xauthority.sh`: the same repair for a host that has not updated yet; run `sudo bash patch_host_1_x11-xauthority.sh --check`, then without `--check`)

- **Test Reports greeted you with a red `N/A`, and CI/CD Reports with a spinner in an empty page (BUG-0744)** — On the first load of `/test-results/reports` the two stat tiles read `N/A%` and `N/A` **in red**, and `/test-results/cicd-reports` showed a lone spinner floating in a blank page and then popped all seven KPI cards, the header and fifteen rows into place at once. The red came from a fallback, not from a fault: with no results yet the rate was the string `'N/A'`, `Number('N/A')` is `NaN`, and every comparison against `NaN` is false, so the rate chain fell through all three branches onto its last one — the error colour. "Not loaded yet" was drawn exactly like "this failed", for as long as the request took. Both pages now draw their own shape while the data lands, from one shared skeleton (`ReportSkeleton.tsx`) so the two pages pulse identically: the tile keeps its label and shows a grey bar for the value, and each table shows placeholder rows built from that table's own column visibility, under its real header, so nothing moves when the data arrives. A load that finishes with genuinely nothing now shows a muted `—`, never a red rate. In plain words: the report pages show a quiet grey placeholder that fits the page instead of a red `N/A` that looks like a failure · [BUG-0744](../bugs/BUG-0744-report-skeleton-loading-state.md) · area: frontend
- **Pokémon runner does not close the gym's chevrons and cannot leave the gym (BUG-0741)** — TASK-33. The run that won the Earth Badge then stood still in the Viridian gym for 300 ticks: the way out ran over the gym's chevron squares, which the runner had learned as one-way hop tiles and closed for ever, so it found no route at all. A chevron is not a push-back tile: the game carries the player one square in the arrow's direction whichever key he presses. The mark now remembers where the carry lands and the grid jumps over the tile the way it jumps over the ledges, so the way out stays open; and the beaten trainer the runner fell back on — his sprite is still in the game although he is gone — is not talked to any more. In plain words: the Pokémon runner walks out of the gym instead of standing in it · [BUG-0741](../bugs/BUG-0741-2026-10-20-pokemon-gym-chevron-is-a-passage-not-a-dead-end.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner does not close the gym's chevron corridor and walk in circles (BUG-0740)** — TASK-33. A gym's chevron is not a push-back tile: it blocks the key the runner pressed and carries him to its landing square a moment later. The key that did not move him left the direction of an earlier step in memory, the carry was read as the game pushing him back, and the square was closed as a one-way hop tile — in the Viridian gym the chevron corridor is the only way to the leader, so the runner was sealed in a pocket with two trainers and walked between two squares for 5 minutes (a local replay of the stalled save: 542 ticks of "walk" and no new square). A key that does not move the player now leaves no direction behind, so a carry is never taken for a push-back, and when the planned key is blocked the runner tries another one — the chevrons carry him through whichever key he presses. A real push-back is still learned: the key that moved the player onto the tile is remembered. In plain words: the Pokémon runner walks through the gym's corridor instead of walking in circles in it · [BUG-0740](../bugs/BUG-0740-2026-10-20-pokemon-gym-chevron-corridor-closed.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner does not seal itself in the Viridian gym (BUG-0738)** — TASK-33. The runner's own step was read as the game pushing the player back off a one-way hop tile: the square the player had come from was marked as a hop tile and closed for ever, and in the Viridian gym that square was the only way out of the pocket the player was standing in — the run stood on one square for 5 minutes (945 "no path" ticks). The runner now remembers the direction of the press that moved the player, so only the game can push him back, a hop that changes map is not a hop, and a hop mark on the plain floor of a map is not believed: the wrong mark already in a saved memory no longer seals the pocket, and a cleared stall forgets the hop tiles like the other way-marks it walled the run in with. In plain words: the Pokémon runner walks out of the gym instead of standing in it · [BUG-0738](../bugs/BUG-0738-2026-10-20-pokemon-viridian-gym-false-hop-tile.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **A new AI endpoint logged its calls with no cost (BUG-0724)** — The second Anthropic key was wired in as its own endpoint, and every call it answered was written to the AI call log with its tokens but no price, so its spend was not counted anywhere. The cost is taken from a table with one row per endpoint and model, and a new endpoint has no row until somebody types or syncs one — here the rows existed only because they had been copied by hand, which is exactly the step that gets forgotten. An endpoint that bills per token now takes the price of another endpoint with the same provider (a second Anthropic key prices like the first one) when it has no price of its own, so a new endpoint never logs "no price". Its own price always wins, another provider's price is never used, and a local endpoint or a subscription plan never borrows one. In plain words: the AI usage page counts the second Anthropic key's spend by itself · [BUG-0724](../bugs/BUG-0724-2026-10-20-new-anthropic-endpoint-logged-no-cost.md) · area: ai
- **Two web scripts on one host no longer destroy each other's browser (BUG-0727)** — TASK-146. Every browser on a host used the same debug port and the same Chrome profile, so the second web script either was refused (`423 device_locked`) or took the port away from the first one: it killed the first run's browser, or attached to it and drove the same tab, and the first run's cleanup then killed the second run's browser instead. Each run now takes a free debug port and a Chrome profile of its own (the host's own service browser keeps port 9222 and the persistent profile), a run's cleanup only touches a browser nobody owns, and two web scripts that drive their own browser run side by side on one host instead of queueing behind each other. In plain words: you can start two web tests on the same host at the same time and neither one kills the other · [BUG-0727](../bugs/BUG-0727-2026-10-20-two-web-scripts-on-one-host-destroyed-each-others-browser.md) · TASK-146 · area: testing
- **A healthy host no longer shows as "error" because its profile needs no image monitoring, archiver or KPI (BUG-0729)** — a host stops the services no device needs (the "pc" profile wants none of them), but the host status still counted them as critical and turned any stopped one into an error, so two working hosts read as broken on the Hosts page right after a deploy. A service that was turned off on purpose is not a failure now; one a device really needs and that is stopped still is. · [BUG-0729](../bugs/BUG-0729-2026-10-20-a-host-whose-profile-needs-no-monitor-archiver-or-kpi-showed-as-error.md) · TASK-146 · area: monitoring
- **A deploy that changed the host code now restarts the host service (BUG-0728)** — the deploy compared the host code of the tree it was about to push with the marker of the last restart, but it computed that id *before* pulling, so it actually compared the previous deploy with the one before it. A deploy that changed the host code pushed the new code and reported success, but the hosts kept running the old code until something else restarted them — a fix could sit on the host and not work. The id is now computed after the pull, so the hosts restart exactly when the host code changed and stay up when it did not. And the fix now reaches the node that runs the deploy: the deploy runs the copy in the node's home directory, not the repo's script, so the repo fix alone changed nothing (the deploy that carried it still skipped the restart). `install_update_core.py` installs the repo's script on a node and keeps that node's fleet targets — the old "scp it over" wiped the node's own target list and made the node deploy to the wrong machines. In plain words: a deploy that ships host code now really restarts the host, and the script that decides it is the one the node runs · [BUG-0728](../bugs/BUG-0728-2026-10-20-a-deploy-that-changed-the-host-code-did-not-restart-the-host-service.md) · TASK-146 · area: platform
- **A host now caps and shows the browser tests it carries (BUG-0743)** — after the browser isolation, every web script brings a Chrome of its own (own debug port, own profile), so the host - not the device - is what runs out, and nothing was counting it. A host now carries at most 6 browser tests at the same time (`VPT_MAX_PARALLEL_BROWSER_RUNS`): the count is taken on the host, in the one place every run passes through, so it cannot be walked around; a run that finds the host full waits for a free slot instead of piling on. The host card shows a "N/6 browsers" chip, and the Run page starts a browser test beside another one instead of queueing it - the server still decides, so a script that is not browser-isolated is queued or refused as before · [BUG-0743](../bugs/BUG-0743-2026-10-21-a-host-could-carry-unlimited-browser-tests-at-once.md) · TASK-150 · area: testing
- **Decision-model AI calls were counted but never traced in Langfuse (BUG-0722)** — TASK-143. A decision call (Jev on OpenRouter) is posted to the Decisions API by hand instead of going through the chat gateway, so it filled the AI call log but left no Langfuse trace: the 15 `text.decision*` usage keys — 4 782 calls in the lab over 30 days — were invisible in the trace list. `_decide` now collects one attempt per hop and sends the same trace the gateway sends (the state and the questions in, the answers out, the routing metadata, the trace id = the call's request id), and the chat fallback is not traced twice. The trace label is the call-log label, so a trace and its `ai_calls` row always agree. · [BUG-0722](../bugs/BUG-0722-2026-10-20-decision-calls-never-traced-in-langfuse.md) · TASK-143 · area: ai
- **Every AI call is logged, and Claude runs only on our Anthropic keys (BUG-0721)** — Monitoring → AI usage now explains every AI dollar. Calls that scripts make on their own (the Localize model bench, the League of Legends relay and review, the Clash of Clans LLM player, the promo voice-over, headless Claude Code runs) and the agent API-key check now write a row like every other call. A failed voice command no longer drops a whole batch of other rows, and per-project spend is stored. Each row now says which hop of the fallback chain answered it. The `agent.browser` rows with 0 tokens were calls OpenRouter refused (HTTP 400, not billed); the reason is now logged. The OpenRouter spend the owner saw came from Claude: Forge ran on Claude through OpenRouter on 2026-10-19 ($7.87). Claude can no longer go through OpenRouter. Every Claude profile now runs on the AngelStreet key, then the Individual key, then MiniMax M3; the Individual step switches on once its workspace id is added. A test fails if new code calls a provider without logging it, and `scripts/ai_calls_audit.py` flags billed calls logged with 0 tokens. ai_calls now matches OpenRouter's own counter: $0.1541 against $0.1536 for the day. In plain words: every AI call is on the usage page, and Claude is always billed to our own keys · [BUG-0721](../bugs/BUG-0721-2026-10-20-ai-calls-not-all-logged-claude-on-openrouter.md) · 🗄 DB migrations `setup/db/migrations/20261020b_ai_calls_hop_index.sql`, `setup/db/migrations/20261020c_claude_anthropic_chain.sql`
- **Pokémon runner lets the Viridian gym's leader fight instead of interrupting him (BUG-0723)** — TASK-33. The runner stepped onto the square the gym's leader looks at while the game was still talking to him: a step cancels the leader's script, his speech starts again from the first page and the fight never came, so the leader was blacklisted with his own speech as the evidence ("Tu ne pourras accéder à la LIGUE POKéMON ... battant notre CHAMPION!"), the gym ran out of candidates and the run stood on one square for 5 minutes. The runner now waits while the game is talking (the leader's fight comes from his own script, about 13 s after the talk), steps into his line of sight once the game is quiet, and only then judges him. · [BUG-0723](../bugs/BUG-0723-2026-10-20-pokemon-viridian-gym-leader-fight-cancelled.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner keeps the Viridian gym's leader and never stands still in a gym (BUG-0719)** — TASK-33. The runner read Giovanni's speech while the game was still writing it (the page was cut in the middle of a word), never read the trainer's battle line that follows it, and took "no battle line" for "he is not a trainer": the gym's real leader was blacklisted, the gym ran out of candidates and the run stood on one square for 5 minutes (533 "at goal" ticks). The answer must now settle before it is judged, a battle takes the person out of the blacklist (the game does not lie about a trainer), the text of a battle is never taken for an answer, and a gym whose candidates are all blacklisted is tried again instead of standing still. · [BUG-0719](../bugs/BUG-0719-2026-10-20-pokemon-viridian-gym-leader-blacklisted-and-idle.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner gets past the Viridian gym's Poké Ball and its hop tiles (BUG-0718)** — TASK-33. The Poké Ball on the gym's floor was taken for the gym leader, and the one-way hop tiles of that gym (the chevron squares that throw the player back down) were walked into for ever: the run stood on one square for 5 minutes. The runner now looks at people only, skips the ones that answer with a plain dialogue instead of a battle (the Pokémon Kid and the Karate Master of that gym) and remembers the hop tiles it was thrown off, so it walks around them. · [BUG-0718](../bugs/BUG-0718-2026-10-20-pokemon-viridian-gym-poke-ball-and-hop-tiles.md) · TASK-33
- **Windows backend host imports cleanly (BUG-0598)** — `backend_host/src/controllers/remote/irtoy.py` was doing `import termios` at module load. `termios` is POSIX-only; on a Windows native install (vpt-windows, any win32 host_vnc) the `from controllers import *` in `app.py` blew up with `No module named 'termios'` before the host ever bound port 6109, so the host never registered and the dashboard saw nothing. The fix gates `select` / `termios` / `tty` behind `sys.platform != "win32"`, exposes a tiny stub for `termios.B115200`, and lets `IRToyRemoteController.__init__` raise `NotImplementedError` on Windows (USB IR Toy is a Linux-only path). The Linux/macOS code path is bit-for-bit unchanged. After the fix + the vpt-windows `.env` was put back to its real config, `getAllHosts` on vpt-pi4 lists `vpt-windows` with `device_count=2` (`Host_Windows` host_vnc + `Mobile_Emulator` android_mobile) and the Dashboard shows both · [BUG-0598](../bugs/BUG-0598-2026-10-17-irtoy-windows-import-error-blocks-host-startup.md) · area: device-control
- **Pokemon runner reaches Saffron via the east gate, not the 5-map Path Route 7 loop (BUG-0607)** — TASK-33. After BUG-0589/0594/0595/0596/0597 made the runner cross Celadon east onto Route 7 (`entry={from:6, side:east, exit:[49,11], map:18}`), `saffron_step` still picked the **south** edge into PATH_ROUTE7 (0x4D) over the **east** edge into ROUTE7_GATE (0x4C) because both are saffron-area dests and the south edge was closer by Manhattan distance. The runner then walked PATH_ROUTE7 → Route 8 west → PATH_ROUTE8 → Route 7 east → ROUTE7_GATE, a 5-map loop that re-landed on Celadon at `(48, 11)` — `lost=[77, 18]` = PATH_ROUTE7 at y=18. Fix: when the runner is on a saffron-area map OTHER than Celadon, group saffron-edges by destination and filter to those whose destination is closest to SAFFRON via the world graph (`world.route(dest, SAFFRON)` hop count). ROUTE7_GATE is 1 hop; PATH_ROUTE7 is 5 hops. The runner walks east → ROUTE7_GATE → into Saffron. Celadon's BUG-0589/0596 partition logic is unchanged. 3 new tests (`test_saffron_step_on_route_7_prefers_east_edge_over_south_edge`, `test_saffron_step_on_path_route_8_walks_north_not_south`, `test_saffron_step_on_celadon_uses_part_logic_not_hop_filter`) using a `_SaffronWorld` fake that returns saffron-area hop distances. Head metric: 112/112 pokemon tests pass · [BUG-0607](../bugs/BUG-0607-2026-10-18-saffron-step-prefers-east-edge-toward-route7-gate.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **A slow web page no longer fails a `navigate_to_url` edge (BUG-0714)** — the page-load limit was the edge's short `wait_time` (2 s); it now has its own timeout (`page_load_timeout`, default 30 s, never below 15 s). In plain words: a page that loads slowly no longer breaks the test · [BUG-0714](../bugs/BUG-0714-2026-10-20-navigate-to-url-load-timeout-was-the-edge-wait-time.md)
- **The Clash of Clans player attacked during the battle countdown, then tapped its own army three times over (BUG-0713)** — TASK-100. The attack harness took the scouting screen for a running battle, and on that screen ("Battle starts in: 29s") a tap on the ground deploys no troop at all, so the first wave of the attack was spent on a countdown. It then re-read the troop tray after every deploy pass and deployed whatever the model said was left; the number on a card only drops when a troop leaves it, never when one dies, so a reading that did not change sent the harness round the loop again — 208 taps for an army of 70, four passes and three model calls per battle, about 90 s of a 3 min battle spent tapping a ground that was already decided. The damage said it plainly: over 65 live battles, 130 taps or fewer ended at 100 % damage, 170 taps or more at 0-29 % and usually a defeat. The harness now waits for the battle to be running (the dark Overall Damage panel) before it deploys, deploys each wave once with a hard cap on the taps, sends the ranged troops in after the tanks have pulled the defenses, and waits for the next village to load before planning it · [BUG-0713](../bugs/BUG-0713-2026-10-20-coc-attack-harness-deployed-during-the-battle-countdown.md) · area: ai

- **The hourly Clash of Clans run lets go of the phone when it is done (BUG-0717)** — TASK-100. The lease that keeps other agents off the phone during the run was taken for 45 minutes and given back only when the run's result arrived. A run now takes 20 to 60 minutes, so the lease ran out in the middle of the game: the registry could only expire it, the phone stayed locked for another 45 minutes after the run was over, and the log said "released" when nothing had been released. The guard now renews the lease while the game is played, gives it back on every way out (including a stop signal), gives it straight back when the hour produced no run at all, and says plainly whether the lease was really released. The platform also stops calling a 60-minute run "timed out" after 30 minutes · [BUG-0717](../bugs/BUG-0717-2026-10-20-coc-hourly-guard-lease-expires-instead-of-being-released.md) · area: platform
- **The Clash of Clans attack stops spending the whole army on a point the game refuses (BUG-0720)** — TASK-100. In half of the battles of the hourly run the harness tapped 140 times and not one troop came out: the game refuses a deploy silently, and the harness could not see it, because its only check asked the model to read the troop numbers on a busy screen it already reads wrong ("71 of 70" for an army of 70). It therefore repeated the same bad point and lost the battle at 0 % damage. The harness now looks at the picture: a troop that appears on the grass changes the pixels around the deploy point, a refused tap does not. It taps four, looks, and only then sends the rest of the wave; when the game refuses a point it tries the next point around it (no model call, no waiting) before asking the model again, and when every point is refused it says so in the report instead of burning the army. The camera does not move when the battle starts, so the point planned on the scouting screen is the point of the battle · [BUG-0720](../bugs/BUG-0720-2026-10-20-coc-attack-taps-deployed-no-troop.md) · area: ai
- **The Clash of Clans phone guard no longer lets go of the phone before the next game (continuation of BUG-0717)** — the guard picked the hour it had to wait for from the moment it started, so a guard started by hand in the middle of an hour (12:53) thought the game of that hour was already over and gave the phone back 31 seconds later, seven minutes before the game of the next hour began. The guard now waits for the trigger that is still ahead of it, and only lets go of the phone once that game had its chance · see BUG-0717 above · area: platform
- **A web navigation tree no longer breaks when the app changes language (BUG-0709)** — a tree built on translated labels (`"TV Guide, Currently selected"`) failed every node check as soon as the app showed German. Web nodes now verify by page URL with the new `waitForUrlToMatch` check (the language segment `/de/` `/en/`, the host and the query are ignored, `*` is a wildcard), `navigate_to_url` accepts `/{lang}/settings` and opens it in the language the app shows now, and Localize recognises a web screen across languages. `build-tree-web` builds this way; the reference web tree was converted (25 nodes; sidebar edges click by position). Edges that click a translated label (profile menu, Search, catalogue tiles, TV Guide controls) are still language-bound. In plain words: switch the app to another language and the tree still finds its screens · [BUG-0709](../bugs/BUG-0709-2026-10-19-web-tree-breaks-when-app-language-changes.md)

- **The Clash of Clans attack stopped dropping its army on building roofs (BUG-0725)** - TASK-100. The 14:00 run lost its three battles at 0 % damage while the report said the troops were deployed: the plan point of the first battle was the roof of the dark elixir storage, and the check that was supposed to notice the refusal compared the field around the point before and after the taps - during a fight the field moves all the time, so the check always said "the troop came out" and the harness sent 59 taps into the roof. The harness now reads two signals that cannot lie, both from the picture and both free: the ground under the plan point must be open grass (a plan that lands on a building is moved to the grass next to it, or sent back to the model with the reason), and the number on the troop card must move - a troop that left the tray changes its number, a refused tap does not. Measured on the recorded frames: a real wave moves 162 to 556 pixels of the badge, a refused battle moves none, and arming a card (which lifts it a pixel for 0.4 s) is over before the picture that opens the comparison is taken. When the number does not move the harness says the game refused the point and tries the next one instead of spending the army on it. The run of 19:00 UTC on the new code fought five battles: two of them dropped the army and won with 100 % damage and 3 stars - the plan of the first one sat on a building, was moved onto the open grass next to it and the wave landed there - and the three the game refused are now reported as refused, with the point that was tried, instead of the harness sending 79 to 103 taps into them · [BUG-0725](../bugs/BUG-0725-2026-10-20-coc-attack-point-on-a-roof-and-the-field-check-lies.md) · area: ai

- **The Clash of Clans phone guard comes back when the box kills it (BUG-0737)** — TASK-100. At 19:03 the guard of the hourly run was terminated from outside (SIGTERM, exit 143) while its run was still on the phone: it let the lease go on its way out and the run carried on driving the phone with nobody holding the lease, so another agent could have taken the same phone at the same time. The unit now restarts a guard that was killed (a clean end - the run finished, the run gave the phone to somebody who was waiting - stays a success and is not restarted), and the guard reads the run's own ledger on the phone on every poll, so a guard that was restarted in the middle of the hour gives the phone back as soon as the run it watches is really over instead of holding it until the next trigger · [BUG-0737](../bugs/BUG-0737-2026-10-20-coc-guard-killed-leaves-its-run-without-a-lease.md) · area: ai

- **The Clash of Clans attack offers the game a whole neighbourhood instead of five spots (BUG-0742)** — TASK-100. Sixteen battles of the runs of 19:00, 20:00, 21:00 and 22:00 UTC: six of the nine defeats ended with 0 % damage and the whole army still in the tray, because the game refused every point the harness tapped - the plan point and four spots 50 px away, all of them in the same patch of grass. The planning prompt asked for a point 60 to 200 px from the Town Hall, which is exactly where the game says no: the points it took were 140 to 355 px away, on the open field around the base. The harness now offers 20 points spread over rings out to 270 px around the plan point, the open ground first, and its second try takes the next points of that list instead of asking the model - a planning call takes four minutes, longer than a battle. The prompt asks for the open grass just outside the base's wall (10 to 50 px from it, at a gap when there is one), a refused point costs two taps instead of four, and a card that carries no number no longer blocks the wave (an empty badge compared with an empty badge always read as "the count did not move"). The runs of 23:00, 00:00 and 01:00 UTC on the new code fought seven battles and the army left the tray in all seven: the battle where the game refused nine points ended with the army deployed, where the old code lost that battle with 0 % damage and 75 troops in the tray, and the three battles fought with the wall-gap prompt were won (52 %, 100 % and 67 % damage) · [BUG-0742](../bugs/BUG-0742-2026-10-20-coc-the-game-refuses-every-point-the-harness-offers.md) · area: ai

- **Pokémon runner goes for Sabrina and Giovanni after Blaine (BUG-0708)** — TASK-33. After the Volcano Badge the runner aimed straight at Victory Road, which no route reached from Cinnabar, and stood lost in Blaine's gym. The story now beats Sabrina, then Giovanni, then takes Route 22 and Route 23 to Victory Road. · [BUG-0708](../bugs/BUG-0708-2026-10-19-pokemon-no-sabrina-giovanni-before-victory-road.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner challenges the gym leader standing in front of it (BUG-0716)** — TASK-33. In Saffron's gym the eight trainers of the other rooms are hidden and one of them has a picture of his own, so the runner took him for the leader and went back and forth on two squares for 5 minutes. It now challenges the person in its own room. · [BUG-0716](../bugs/BUG-0716-2026-10-20-pokemon-gym-leader-hidden-trainer.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner crosses Viridian Forest on the way to Giovanni (BUG-0715)** — TASK-33. Back from Saffron by Diglett's Cave, the runner went in and out of the forest's north gate for 5 minutes. It now walks through the forest to Viridian. · [BUG-0715](../bugs/BUG-0715-2026-10-20-pokemon-earth-badge-forest-gate-loop.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner reaches Sabrina through her gym's teleporters (BUG-0712)** — TASK-33. Sabrina's room in Saffron's gym is reached only by teleporter pads, so the runner stood in the entrance room for 5 minutes. It now finds the chain of pads that leads to her room and steps on them in turn. · [BUG-0712](../bugs/BUG-0712-2026-10-19-pokemon-sabrina-gym-teleporter-pads.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner reaches Giovanni in Silph Co. (BUG-0711)** — TASK-33. Silph Co.'s stairs end in a part of 11F with no way to Giovanni, so the runner stood there for 5 minutes. It now fetches the Card Key on 5F (making room in a full bag), opens the locked gates and takes the teleporters from 3F to 7F to 11F, beats Giovanni and gets the president's Master Ball. · [BUG-0711](../bugs/BUG-0711-2026-10-19-pokemon-silph-co-11f-card-key.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner frees Silph Co. before Sabrina (BUG-0710)** — TASK-33. Rocket grunts block Saffron's gym door until Giovanni is beaten in Silph Co., so the runner went in and out of Route 6's gate for 5 minutes. It now climbs Silph Co.'s stairs first, and Route 6's gate no longer sends it round in a circle. · [BUG-0710](../bugs/BUG-0710-2026-10-19-pokemon-saffron-gym-blocked-until-silph-co.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner moves on after beating Blaine (BUG-0707)** — TASK-33. The Volcano Badge was only counted once the save held 7 badges, but the story skips Sabrina, so Blaine's badge is the 6th: the runner kept walking back to Blaine. The milestone now checks the Volcano Badge itself. · [BUG-0707](../bugs/BUG-0707-2026-10-19-pokemon-volcano-badge-counted-as-seventh.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner is no longer stopped in the middle of a gym leader battle (BUG-0706)** — TASK-33. The stall detector counted battle time as "no progress", so Blaine's long fight used up its 5 minutes and the run was stopped mid-battle. The first 15 minutes of one battle no longer count; a fight stuck longer still does. · [BUG-0706](../bugs/BUG-0706-2026-10-19-pokemon-stall-mid-gym-leader-battle.md) · TASK-33
- **The Localize page shows the right numbers (BUG-0704)** — on the Localize page the average score read 0.00 although runs were named, the per-device squares said 60 % on a 41 % device, and the close-call share was green and unlabelled. The average now comes from the whole time window, each device's squares show its own rate, and close calls read "x of y named" in amber. In plain words: the numbers on the Localize page can be trusted · [BUG-0704](../bugs/BUG-0704-2026-10-19-localize-matches-tiles-wrong-numbers.md)
- **Trees built by an AI agent are laid out instead of stacked on one spot (BUG-0702)** — nodes created through the MCP tools all started at (0, 0) and an edited node was reset there and lost its screenshot, so an agent-built tree opened in the Navigation Editor as one spot. `update_node` now keeps a node's stored position and screenshot, and a new `auto_layout_tree` tool, called at the end of every tree-building skill, lays the tree out top to bottom. In plain words: an agent-built map opens readable, with its screenshots · [BUG-0702](../bugs/BUG-0702-2026-10-19-agent-built-tree-nodes-stacked-at-origin.md)
- **A web click by text no longer fails on an off-screen twin (BUG-0701)** ��� on a Flutter web app the first label match could be hidden or zero-size and the click gave up although a later match was visible; it now clicks the first match that has a box. In plain words: fewer failed clicks when building web maps. · [BUG-0701](../bugs/BUG-0701-2026-10-19-web-click-dump-fallback-skips-boxless-match.md)
- **Screenshots work again on web hosts after a restart (BUG-0695)** — since web hosts default to the `pc` monitoring profile, the capture kept every frame while the clean-up service idled, so the host's capture memory filled about 30 minutes after a restart and every screenshot (and node screenshot) failed. The capture now follows the same profile and keeps only the newest frames. · [BUG-0695](../bugs/BUG-0695-2026-10-19-host-vnc-hot-tmpfs-fills-archiver-idle-stream-full.md)
- **"Use fingerprint" now replaces the node's verifications (BUG-0692)** — the toggle in Edit node was ignored when the node also had authored verifications; navigation and Run still ran them. With the switch on (and a fingerprint captured), the fingerprint check runs and the authored verifications are kept but skipped. · [BUG-0692](../bugs/BUG-0692-2026-10-19-use-fingerprint-ignored.md) · area: testing

- **Verification and action lists load for a host on another server (BUG-0691)** — Edit Node and the action pickers showed only the node's existing verifications for a device owned by another server, because the schemas were asked from the primary server ("Host not found"). They now come from the host's own server, and so do the Edge / Node editor's run, capture, save and Go To calls. · [BUG-0691](../bugs/BUG-0691-2026-10-19-schemas-wrong-server.md) · area: platform

- **A calm live channel keeps its "live" badge (BUG-0688)** — on a studio channel where only the presenter moves, Localize could not name the screen between two bursts of motion and the badge vanished for several seconds. When it cannot name the screen, it now stays on "live" for up to 20 s after the last time it saw the live picture; a real screen, popup or no-signal still replaces it at once. A talking presenter (small movement only) or sound over a moving picture also counts as live when the screen cannot be named. A phone device needs a screen set in its host settings for Localize to run at all (done for the Clash of Clans phone). In plain words: live TV no longer blinks out. · [BUG-0688](../bugs/BUG-0688-2026-10-19-calm-live-channel-flips-to-unknown.md)
- **Faster, fewer failed steps when an agent builds a web tree (BUG-0684)** — on a Flutter web app a click by text took about 18 s, because five selectors each waited 3 s before the right search; it now skips the ones that are not on the page. A click also takes an XPath or a bare element id, a script with `return` runs, an edge update keeps its endpoints, and the agent's device tools no longer fail when it forgets the host name. In plain words: map building on web apps wastes far fewer steps. · [BUG-0684](../bugs/BUG-0684-2026-10-19-web-tree-build-tool-errors-and-slow-flutter-click.md)
- **A queued script keeps its userinterface (BUG-0685)** — a script started on a busy device was queued and then stopped with "Unknown argument(s)" because its `--userinterface=<name>` was dropped when the queue started it. · [BUG-0685](../bugs/BUG-0685-2026-10-19-queued-script-run-loses-userinterface-flag.md)
- **Scripts no longer run on a device someone else has under Take Control (BUG-0686)** — API-key and auto-sign callers all share one built-in user id, which let a CI smoke script run under another session's Take Control. That id no longer counts as "the same user"; the smoke check skips such a device (shown as SKIP) instead of queueing. · [BUG-0686](../bugs/BUG-0686-2026-10-19-ci-smoke-runs-under-another-take-control.md)
- **Web hosts no longer fill a saved login into the app under test (BUG-0687)** — the host browser's password manager and autofill are switched off at launch, so a login page opens with empty fields. · [BUG-0687](../bugs/BUG-0687-2026-10-19-host-chrome-autofills-saved-login.md)
- **Build Map no longer freezes the host (BUG-0682)** — an AI exploration step (Build Map / auto-discover, validate next item, node verification) ran its device navigation directly on the host's request thread and could stop the whole host from answering for as long as it ran (30 min on host-clone-2). These steps now run on a worker thread, so streams and other requests keep working while Build Map explores. · [BUG-0682](../bugs/BUG-0682-2026-10-19-auto-discover-asyncio-run-wedges-host.md)
- **Localize reads live TV right after a channel change** — pressing channel up or down could leave the screen "unknown" for a moment (the picture was held or the ~3 s programme banner split the motion window); Localize now looks again, up to two fresh windows about 1 s apart, and answers `live` when one of them moves. An unknown verdict also carries the motion it measured.
- **Loading spinners keep turning with "Reduce motion" on (BUG-0680)** — the reduced-motion setting (or the operating system's) stopped every spinner, so a loading screen looked frozen; spinners now keep turning while page transitions and chart animations stay off. · [BUG-0680](../bugs/BUG-0680-2026-10-19-spinner-frozen-when-reduce-motion-on.md)

- **force_unlock no longer kills other callers' runs** — an agent that retried a busy device with `force_unlock=true` used to SIGTERM whatever ran there, including CI and other users' scripts. force_unlock now clears only a stale lock or your own; another caller's live script, deployment or testcase is refused with `device_busy` (HTTP 409, "device busy: <reason> by <owner> since <t>"), so the agent waits or queues. The UI Force Takeover and `/forceUnlock` for humans are unchanged. Server deploy only. In plain words: an agent can no longer stop someone else's test by passing force_unlock · [BUG-0679](../bugs/BUG-0679-2026-10-19-force-unlock-aborts-other-callers-runs.md) · area: platform
- **The quality-switch loading screen no longer sticks with WebRTC on (BUG-0678)** — after switching LOW / SD / HD in the stream modal with WEB on, the "Loading … quality stream" spinner stayed up although the new stream was already running; it now clears a few seconds after the restarted stream is confirmed. · [BUG-0678](../bugs/BUG-0678-2026-10-19-quality-switch-overlay-stuck-webrtc.md)

- **The League bot no longer reads game-chat lines as enemy minions (BUG-0677)** — red bot names and "has slain" lines in the chat looked like health bars (about one false bar per frame at the left edge); the chat area is now ignored. · [BUG-0677](../bugs/BUG-0677-2026-10-19-lol-chat-text-read-as-minion-bars.md)
- **Stream quality switch works for devices on another server (BUG-0676)** — with "All servers" selected, clicking SD / HD on a device owned by a peer server showed "Failed to switch to SD quality" (the primary server answered "Host not found"); the quality calls now go to the server that owns the host. · [BUG-0676](../bugs/BUG-0676-2026-10-19-stream-quality-switch-wrong-server-all-servers.md)

- **The Localize badge follows the picture again (BUG-0675)** — on a host without a local Redis (vpt-mf1) every Localize run waited 26 s to hand its answer to a queue, and all devices of the host waited with it, so a box back from a reboot kept showing "Black Screen" for a minute while it was on Home. The hand-over now runs in the background. In plain words: the badge changes within a second or two of the screen changing, and it shows the screen name only ("Home", not "Home (tab bar hidden)"). · [BUG-0675](../bugs/BUG-0675-2026-10-19-localize-badge-stale-redis-push-blocks-worker.md)
- **Recurring unknown screens now get a name (BUG-0674)** — a screen Localize could not name but kept seeing (for example the operator Home with its tab bar hidden, full-screen poster) was never named: the stored picture had rotated away on the host, the naming job ran once a night and only wrote a suggestion nobody accepted. The server now keeps a small picture of every unknown screen, the job runs every hour and writes the name on the screen itself, and the operator Home with the tab bar hidden is named "Home (tab bar hidden)". In plain words: a screen seen again and again gets its name within the hour. · [BUG-0674](../bugs/BUG-0674-2026-10-19-recurring-unknown-screens-never-named.md)
- **A queued run of a script named without `.py` no longer fails (BUG-0672)** — when the device was busy, `vpt/smoke_ui` and similar names queued through the API were rejected as "no DB campaign found", making the vpt-smoke CI job flake; the scheduler now runs them as the script file they are · [BUG-0672](../bugs/BUG-0672-2026-10-19-queued-file-script-without-py-fails-as-campaign.md) · area: platform
- **The League bot now sees enemy minions that are about to die (BUG-0671)** — health bars under about 13 % were not detected at all, so the minions one auto from dying were invisible and ignored; they are read again. · [BUG-0671](../bugs/BUG-0671-2026-10-19-lol-bars-under-13-percent-invisible.md)
- **League bot runs no longer leave screen recorders running (BUG-0670)** — a crashed or killed Garen run kept its `ffmpeg` desktop recorder going for 10+ hours; every run now stops its recorder in a `finally` (finalising the video) and removes leftovers of earlier runs at start. · [BUG-0670](../bugs/BUG-0670-2026-10-19-lol-orphaned-gdigrab-recorders.md)
- **A screen Localize cannot name no longer gets a badge, and its seen count is real** — the live video tile and the monitoring overlay show nothing instead of "unknown" / "Known screen (unknown, seen 3x)". Screens Localize shows again are now counted once per sighting (not only when an alert fired), so the unnamed-screens backlog reflects how often they really appear. · [BUG-0663](../bugs/BUG-0663-2026-10-19-unnamed-known-screen-badge-and-seen-count.md)

- **A dark live picture is no longer called a Black Screen (BUG-0661)** — Localize and the incident detector now call a frame black only when the whole picture is uniformly dark; a dark scene with a logo, a lit performer or a bright floor stays live. Real standby frames are still black. · [BUG-0661](../bugs/BUG-0661-2026-10-19-dark-live-scene-called-black-screen.md)
- **STB tiles on Device Control start faster** — the 5 fps STB streams had a keyframe only in every other 0.4 s segment, so a tile that joined on the wrong one failed to decode and reloaded before showing video. The encoder now puts a keyframe at the start of every segment at any frame rate. · [BUG-0660](../bugs/BUG-0660-2026-10-19-stream-gop-every-other-segment-at-5fps.md)

- **Windows-only deploy no longer queues behind the whole node** — `update_core.sh --host-windows` claimed the node env (`node1-vpt/*`) and waited behind unrelated device leases on the Linux hosts. It now claims `vpt-windows/*` (override with `WIN_LEASE_ENV`) and nothing on the node env; with `--server` or `--host` as well it claims both. Operators: the proxmox copy of `update_core.sh` must be refreshed for this to apply.
- **Renaming, deleting and managing members of a project works again (BUG-0659)** — these actions failed with a server error for everyone because a permission check lost its name in a refactor. The admin-or-project-owner rule is unchanged. · [BUG-0659](../bugs/BUG-0659-2026-10-19-projects-write-routes-nameerror.md)
- **Creating a project works again (BUG-0658)** — creating a project failed with "Failed to create project" because the server stored the wrong kind of id as its creator. It now stores the creator's profile id. · [BUG-0658](../bugs/BUG-0658-2026-10-19-project-create-fk.md)
- **Pokémon runner answers the quiz in Blaine's gym (BUG-0705)** — TASK-33. Blaine stands behind six gates that open on quiz answers; the runner walked into a wall at the door for minutes. It now answers each quiz machine in turn and reaches Blaine. · [BUG-0705](../bugs/BUG-0705-2026-10-19-pokemon-cinnabar-gym-quiz-gates.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner no longer walks back and forth in the Mansion basement (BUG-0703)** — TASK-33. An item ball already picked up looked like it was still on the floor while it was just off screen, so the runner stepped toward it, saw it gone, and stepped back, for minutes. It now remembers every ball it has picked up and only forgets one it sees on the floor. · [BUG-0703](../bugs/BUG-0703-2026-10-19-pokemon-mansion-taken-ball-offscreen.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner makes room in a full bag for the Secret Key (BUG-0699)** — TASK-33. With twenty kinds of items in the bag, the Secret Key's ball in the Mansion's basement had no room, and the runner stood next to it. It now throws away a Nugget first, then picks up the key. · [BUG-0699](../bugs/BUG-0699-2026-10-19-pokemon-mansion-full-bag.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner heals on Cinnabar Island before the Mansion (BUG-0698)** — TASK-33. The runner did not know the Pokémon Centers on Cinnabar Island and in Saffron, so before the Mansion it walked all the way back to Viridian to heal and got lost there. It now heals at the nearest Center, Cinnabar's. · [BUG-0698](../bugs/BUG-0698-2026-10-19-pokemon-cinnabar-center.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner fetches the Secret Key before Blaine's gym (BUG-0697)** — TASK-33. Blaine's gym door on Cinnabar Island is locked until the Secret Key from the Pokémon Mansion's basement; the runner walked into the locked door for five minutes until the save was locked. It now goes through the Mansion first (stairs, switches and the hole down to the basement), takes the key and walks back out to the gym. · [BUG-0697](../bugs/BUG-0697-2026-10-19-pokemon-cinnabar-gym-secret-key.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner goes through Route 7's gate house again (BUG-0696)** — TASK-33. Of the gate's two door squares on the Saffron side only one opens; the runner kept stepping on the other for five minutes until the save was locked. A door square that does not open after every way was tried twice is now skipped for its twin. · [BUG-0696](../bugs/BUG-0696-2026-10-19-pokemon-route7-gate-dead-door.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner goes to Pallet Town through Diglett's Cave (BUG-0694)** — TASK-33. The way back west from Cerulean is a one-way ledge, so the runner wandered into a dead end south of Cerulean until the save was locked. It now leaves Route 12 west by Route 11 and Diglett's Cave, and clearing a stall also forgets the exits the stalled run had marked as blocked. · [BUG-0694](../bugs/BUG-0694-2026-10-19-pokemon-route4-ledge.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner reaches Cinnabar by Pallet Town and Route 21 (BUG-0693)** — TASK-33. Route 20 turned out to be two seas joined only through the Seafoam Islands, so the runner walked in and out of the cave until the save was locked. It now walks back to Pallet Town and surfs Route 21 south to Cinnabar Island. · [BUG-0693](../bugs/BUG-0693-2026-10-19-pokemon-route20-split.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner surfs past the Seafoam Islands to Cinnabar (BUG-0690)** — TASK-33. On the third basement of the Seafoam Islands the runner tried a boulder puzzle with made-up positions and stood still until the run stopped and the save was locked. The puzzle only leads to Articuno, so the runner now surfs Route 20 to Cinnabar Island instead. · [BUG-0690](../bugs/BUG-0690-2026-10-19-pokemon-seafoam-boulder-stall.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner no longer goes up and down one Seafoam ladder (BUG-0689)** — TASK-33. On the first basement of the Seafoam Islands the runner took the nearest ladder down, which leads into a dead-end corner, climbed back up and took the same ladder again until the run stopped and the save was locked. It now takes the ladder into the part of the floor below that leads on. · [BUG-0689](../bugs/BUG-0689-2026-10-19-pokemon-seafoam-ladder-pingpong.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner leaves Fuchsia's pond by the Good Rod house's front door (BUG-0683)** — TASK-33. After fishing its Surf learner at the pond behind the fishing guru's house, the runner walked into the house and straight back out the same back door, over and over, so it never reached Route 19 and the save was locked. Inside the house it now heads on to Route 19 through the front door. · [BUG-0683](../bugs/BUG-0683-2026-10-19-pokemon-pond-house-pingpong.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner surfs from Fuchsia to the Seafoam Islands (BUG-0681)** — TASK-33. Once its Pokémon knew Surf, the runner still could not cross water and its route to Seafoam started on Cinnabar, so it stood at Fuchsia until the run stopped and the save was locked. Water now counts as a way when Surf is known, the runner uses Surf from the menu at the shore, and the route goes Fuchsia, Route 19, Route 20. · [BUG-0681](../bugs/BUG-0681-2026-10-19-pokemon-no-field-surf-lost-at-fuchsia.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner fishes a Surf learner instead of ending the story at 5 badges (BUG-0673)** — TASK-33. After the Safari Zone hunt gave up, nothing ever looked for a Pokémon that can learn Surf again, so every run ended "story done" after one tick and the long play was locked. A new step gets the Good Rod from the fishing guru in Fuchsia, fishes from the nearest shore and catches the Goldeen or Poliwag that bites (at any party size), then teaches it Surf. A skipped goal whose move the party has learned since (Strength, then Surf) is un-skipped, so Seafoam, Cinnabar and Victory Road are played instead of passed over. · [BUG-0673](../bugs/BUG-0673-2026-10-19-pokemon-no-surf-learner-after-safari-hunt.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner ends the story instead of stalling at earth_badge without Surf (BUG-0662)** — TASK-33. With no Surf user the runner could not cross Route 23 to Victory Road and stalled 10 times in Cerulean City. `earth_badge` is now deferred when Surf is, like Seafoam and Cinnabar, so the run ends with "story done" and saves; such a run no longer counts as a no-progress run. · [BUG-0662](../bugs/BUG-0662-2026-10-19-earth-badge-unreachable-without-surf.md) · TASK-33
