- **Pokémon runner walls a guard after his third talk and stops talking past the cap (BUG-0657)** — TASK-33. A guard in the only way out was talked to up to four times and never made a wall, and the route path counted its talks separately. The runner now walls a guard or blocker on his third talk, on both paths, with one talk count per NPC. 1 new regression test in the pokemon test file. · [BUG-0657](../bugs/BUG-0657-2026-10-19-guard-wall-on-cap.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner stops alternating between two guards (BUG-0656)** — TASK-33. Once the runner stood next to a guard, its target was dropped, because that guard's sprite is hidden at close range, and the runner walked to the other guard, then back. The runner now keeps the adjacent guard as its target and talks to him until the talk cap. · [BUG-0656](../bugs/BUG-0656-2026-10-19-guard-adjacent-pingpong.md) · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner logs when its walk target changes (BUG-0655)** — TASK-33. A stall where the runner alternated between two walk targets could not be read from the log. The runner now records one `walk_target` event when its target or the branch that chose it changes, with the map, position, target and branch. Diagnostic only: no change to movement. 1 new test in the pokemon test file. In plain words: the stall log now shows which target the runner walked to and why · [BUG-0655](../bugs/BUG-0655-2026-10-19-walk-target-change-log.md) · TASK-33 · area: entertainment
- **Pokémon runner no longer cycles an NPC's dialogue forever (BUG-0653)** — TASK-33. A dialogue that kept showing text boxes made the runner press A on every tick and never move, and nothing counted it. After 60 text ticks in a row the runner now logs one `text_stuck` event and presses B twice. · [BUG-0653](../bugs/BUG-0653-2026-10-19-text-loop-cap.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner stops ping-ponging between two squares while it walks to a guard (BUG-0651)** — TASK-33. With no way out of its part of a map, the runner walks to the nearest person it has not talked to. A person is hidden once the runner is within 5 squares, so the nearest one changed every tick and the runner stepped back and forth. The chosen guard is now kept until the runner is next to him, he is out of reach or the talk limit is reached. 1 new regression test in the pokemon test file. In plain words: the runner no longer walks back and forth between two squares when it has to talk to a guard · [BUG-0651](../bugs/BUG-0651-2026-10-19-guard-target-flip.md) · TASK-33 · area: entertainment
- **Pokémon runner presses A in a cutscene that has no text box (BUG-0648)** — TASK-33. A cutscene that locked the controls without drawing a text box made the runner wait forever. After about 5 s of that it now presses A once, and then again every 5 s until the controls unlock. · [BUG-0648](../bugs/BUG-0648-2026-10-19-cutscene-stuck-press.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner logs its walk decision when a walk stalls (BUG-0646)** — TASK-33. A stall report said only that the runner had not moved. After four failed steps in a row the runner now records one `walk_stuck` event with the map, position, target, edge and the size of the reachable part. Diagnostic only: no change to movement. 1 new test. · [BUG-0646](../bugs/BUG-0646-2026-10-19-walk-stall-log.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner no longer loops through a house on the way to Victory Road (BUG-0644)** — TASK-33. The route search could leave the player's part of a map, walk through a house and come back into that same part, which got around the first-hop rules; the runner then went out of the house onto the square it had come from and back, for five minutes. A route now stays out of the part it starts in. In plain words: the runner stops walking in circles at Victory Road's approach. · [BUG-0644](../bugs/BUG-0644-2026-10-18-victory-road-route-loop-through-house.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner stops recursing when a dead-end pocket has nothing new to mark (BUG-0642)** — after BUG-0639 a scheduled run could end with `maximum recursion depth exceeded` right after navigation: the dead-end walk-back called itself through the route search with no new exit to mark. It now stops when there is nothing new to mark. · [BUG-0642](../bugs/BUG-0642-2026-10-18-retreat-recursion.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokémon runner skips Seafoam and Cinnabar when no party member can learn Surf or Strength (BUG-0643)** — TASK-33. With Surf and Strength both deferred, no route reaches the islands, and the runner stood still in the Saffron dojo until the stall detector stopped it. Those two milestones are now deferred too, so the story moves on. A prize Pokémon's Pokédex page (the dojo's Hitmonlee), which the runner did not recognise and walked against, is now closed with A. · [BUG-0643](../bugs/BUG-0643-2026-10-18-pokemon-seafoam-deferred-without-surf.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner takes Route 5's gate house door instead of getting lost south of it (BUG-0640)** — TASK-33. On the way from Saffron to Cerulean the runner read the strip south of the Route 5 gate house as a dead end, because it had never entered the gate house, and ended with no route. A part of a map with no known way on now first tries a door into a map not visited yet. · [BUG-0640](../bugs/BUG-0640-2026-10-18-pokemon-route5-pocket-gate-door.md) · TASK-33
- **Pokémon runner stops ping-ponging between Saffron and Route 5 (BUG-0639)** — TASK-33. In the Seafoam milestone the runner walked north from Saffron onto Route 5, landed in its dead-end south half and walked back, for 30 minutes. A dead end entered over an edge is now marked, and its exits are no longer the first hop of a route, so the runner takes the known way round. · [BUG-0639](../bugs/BUG-0639-2026-10-18-saffron-to-seafoam-stall.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Killed script finalisation no longer leaves a run unfinished (BUG-0638)** — a script writes its verdict before video/report/R2 finalisation, and stale deployment cleanup now marks the linked script result failed too, so later scheduled fires are not blocked by an orphaned run. · [BUG-0638](../bugs/BUG-0638-2026-10-18-stale-deployment-leaves-script-result-running.md) · area: testing
- **YouTube devices have sound (BUG-0637)** — a device fed from a YouTube link played video only: the host picked YouTube's video-only stream. It now fetches the matching audio stream too and adds it to the device stream, so the overlay shows audio and the player has sound. · [BUG-0637](../bugs/BUG-0637-2026-10-18-youtube-device-no-audio.md) · area: monitoring
- **vpt-pi4 tiles no longer poll the wrong server (BUG-0636)** — with "All servers" selected, the Tello drone tile and the Localize badge of a vpt-pi4 device asked the main server, which answered 400 "Host not found" every few seconds, and the drone tile could not see the drone. They now ask the server that owns the host. · [BUG-0636](../bugs/BUG-0636-2026-10-18-pi4-tiles-400-host-not-found.md) · area: device-control
- **Pokemon runner stops retrying a dead Celadon mart door (BUG-0635)** — TASK-33. The BUG-0634 retry dropped the mark on the one door to the roof and walked into it again and again. A door that fails after its one retry is now dead for the run, so the runner takes another route or stops with no route. 1 new test. · [BUG-0635](../bugs/BUG-0635-2026-10-18-warp-retry-capped.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing

- **Long validation runs are no longer killed at 60 minutes (BUG-0633)** — a full `stb_tv` validation needs over an hour on a slow box and was stopped at exactly 3600 s with no result saved. Validation now has a 4-hour limit; other scripts keep one hour. In plain words: long validation runs finish and leave their report. · [BUG-0633](../bugs/BUG-0633-2026-10-18-validation-killed-at-one-hour-no-result.md)
- **Stream, VNC and monitoring tiles load on every server in 'All servers' mode (BUG-0631)** — tiles of hosts owned by another server stayed on "Loading stream..." and kept retrying, because their stream, VNC and monitoring requests went to the selected server, which does not know the host. They now go to the owning server. · [BUG-0631](../bugs/BUG-0631-2026-10-18-all-servers-stream-vnc-monitoring-wrong-server.md) · area: platform
- **League relaunch recovers from a dead client, and a practice game can be left cleanly** — the phase-0 kill step now also removes leftover League/Riot client processes, so a client stuck on "lost connection" no longer blocks the next run; `scripts/lol_practice/exit_game.py` leaves a Practice Tool game through Esc → Exit Game → Leave (and refuses on any real match). · [BUG-0630](../bugs/BUG-0630-2026-10-18-lol-lost-connection-leftover-client-processes.md)

- **Pokemon runner no longer loops through the Underground Path before Saffron (BUG-0629)** — TASK-33. Until the runner has visited Saffron, the world graph does not know a route to it, so the BUG-0607 hop filter could not tell the saffron-edges apart and the closest one (the south edge into PATH_ROUTE7) won. The runner looped Celadon → Route 7 → Underground Path → Route 8 → Celadon about every 85 s. Route 7's gate to Saffron (ROUTE7_GATE) is now the edge chosen while no route is known. 1 new test; pokemon tests green. · [BUG-0629](../bugs/BUG-0629-2026-10-18-celadon-saffron-guide-skip-loop.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokemon runner takes the Route 7 gate to Saffron, not the loop through Path Route 7 (BUG-0632)** — TASK-33. Route 7's east connection leads to Saffron, but the gate house is a warp in the middle of Route 7, so no saffron-edge was left to walk to. The runner then followed the shortest known route to Saffron, a 16-hop loop through Path Route 7 and Route 8 back to Celadon. While the gate is unvisited, the runner now walks to its west door. 1 new test; pokemon tests green. · [BUG-0632](../bugs/BUG-0632-2026-10-18-route7-saffron-cycle.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **Pokemon runner no longer stands still at Celadon (39, 20) before Saffron (BUG-0634)** — TASK-33. The saffron drink was no longer in the bag, but the runner still believed it had bought one, so it never bought another and kept walking into the Celadon mart door that an earlier stall had marked unusable. The stale flag is cleared on Celadon, and a door that is the only way on is retried instead of walked into forever. 1 new test. · [BUG-0634](../bugs/BUG-0634-2026-10-18-celadon-39-20-same-spot.md) · TASK-33 · area: testing
- **League launcher no longer flashes console windows** — on `vpt-windows` the phase-0 and practice runners looked up and killed processes by spawning `tasklist` / `taskkill` / `cmd`, and each spawn flashed a console window on the desktop (every 5 s during waits). They now use `psutil` in-process and `os.startfile`, so nothing is spawned. · [BUG-0628](../bugs/BUG-0628-2026-10-18-phase0-console-windows-flash.md)
- **Take Control works on every server in 'All servers' mode (BUG-0625)** — Take Control and Release on a host owned by another server failed with "Host not found", because the request went to the primary server. It now goes to the server that owns the host. The same applies to running scripts and test cases, AI test generation and scheduling a run from Run Tests and the Test Builder. · [BUG-0625](../bugs/BUG-0625-2026-10-18-take-control-wrong-server.md) · area: platform
- **Stuck 'running' deployment rows are cleared after a host restart (BUG-0624)** — a deployment row left `running` when the host restarted kept its device busy, so every recurring CRON fire was skipped as "host busy" for up to an hour. The startup clean-up asked for the executions of all ~1000 deployments on the host in one URL, which the database refused (HTTP 414), so it never ran. It now filters by host through the deployment join, the same way the rest of the scheduler does, and the host's busy-device check uses the same filter. · [BUG-0624](../bugs/BUG-0624-2026-10-18-stale-running-rows-after-restart.md) · TASK-33 · area: platform
- **Dashboard numbers open the runs they count (BUG-0614)** — a click on Failures 30 opened an empty Test Reports list: the page filtered only the latest 100 runs. The status and time range now go to the server, so Failures, Passed and Aborted open exactly those runs; a host row opens Device Control on that host's devices, and the device shortcut icons open in a new tab. · [BUG-0614](../bugs/BUG-0614-2026-10-18-dashboard-failures-link-opens-empty.md) · area: platform
- **Hosts and devices lists every server under All servers (BUG-0613)** — with All servers picked, Dashboard → Hosts and devices said "No servers connected" because it looked for a server named after the All servers choice. It now shows one card per server, and a click on a server's title folds that card away (remembered per browser). · [BUG-0613](../bugs/BUG-0613-2026-10-18-hosts-devices-all-servers-empty.md) · area: platform
- **Endpoint, model, user, usage and project AI budgets save again (BUG-0612)** — only team budgets could be saved since the finer-bucket budgets landed: the old `team_id` column was still required, so every other budget failed in the database. The column is gone and the owning team (`team_scope`) is now required. · [BUG-0612](../bugs/BUG-0612-2026-10-18-ai-budgets-team-id-not-null.md) · 🗄 DB migration `20261018f_ai_budgets_drop_team_id.sql`
- **Windows desktop audio now reaches the dashboard (BUG-0605)** — the capture was live on the host but the browser logged `Audio Stream URL received: <none>`: the server-side `av/getStreamUrl` shortcut built only `stream_url`, and `buildAudioStreamUrl` doubled the host prefix and the `segments` folder for an absolute path (`/host/x/host/x/.../segments/segments/output.m3u8`). Both are fixed; the response now carries `audio_stream_url`, so the `Host_Windows` tile renders its audio track and the volume chip · [BUG-0605](../bugs/BUG-0605-2026-10-17-vpt-windows-audio-url-missing-from-getstreamurl.md) · area: device-control
- **vpt-windows desktop audio is live and audible again (BUG-0604)** — the capture ran as SYSTEM in session 0 and recorded digital silence (−90 dB), the rolling `raw.pcm` was replayed from byte 0 on every ffmpeg restart (65 s loop, 425 MB and growing ~14 GB/day), and the wrapper only watched process liveness so a silent PortAudio stream was never restarted. The pipeline is now one OS pipe (`python capture | ffmpeg -i -`, no file), captures **WASAPI loopback of the default speaker** so it follows whatever endpoint is playing, runs as the console user in session 1, and restarts itself when the playlist stops advancing. Measured on vpt-windows: −26 dB mean / −13 dB peak while a system sound plays (was −90 dB). In plain words: the Windows desktop tile's audio now carries what the machine is playing · [BUG-0604](../bugs/BUG-0604-2026-10-17-vpt-windows-desktop-audio-stalled-and-silent.md) · area: device-control
- **The Windows host installer parses again under PowerShell 5.1** — `setup/local/windows/backend_host/install_host_windows.ps1` carried 15 em dashes and two `é` without a BOM (PowerShell 5.1 reads a BOM-less script as ANSI, and the em dash's `0x94` byte closes a string) plus a stray `}` left behind by the BUG-0600 hardening, so the file was rejected before any function ran and only the hand-maintained snapshots at the repo root were usable. The file is now pure ASCII and the extra brace is gone; the Windows PowerShell 5.1 parser on `vpt-windows` reports 0 errors. In plain words: installing or repairing a Windows host runs the maintained script again · [BUG-0603](../bugs/BUG-0603-2026-10-17-install-host-windows-ps1-does-not-parse.md) · area: platform
- **vpt-windows `Mobile_Emulator` tile no longer goes black and reloads** — a rebuilt `.env` lost `DEVICE2_VIDEO_FPS`, so `run_ffmpeg.ps1` passed an empty string to `Start-Process -ArgumentList`, ffmpeg never started, and the last `output.m3u8` (with `#EXT-X-ENDLIST` and already-deleted segments) was still served to hls.js, which 404'd and retried in a loop. The script now defaults a missing FPS to 5 with a warning, and the host `.env` has the key back. In plain words: the emulator tile plays again instead of showing a black, reloading player · [BUG-0601](../bugs/BUG-0601-2026-10-17-vpt-windows-emulator-tile-black-reloading-missing-fps.md) · area: device-control
- **vpt-windows `Host_Windows` tile shows the desktop instead of a black canvas** — TightVNC 2.8.90's screen-hook capture returned an all-black framebuffer on this Windows 11 + AMD desktop while the desktop itself rendered fine. The installer now sets `UseHooks=0`, `PollFullScreen=1`, `UseMirrorDriver=0` (GDI full-screen polling) alongside `AllowLoopback=1`, and the host's `tvnserver` registry was patched and the service restarted. In plain words: the Windows desktop tile shows the real screen · [BUG-0602](../bugs/BUG-0602-2026-10-17-vpt-windows-host-vnc-black-framebuffer.md) · area: device-control
- **Windows VNC desktop fits the screen and shows PyAutoGUI controls** — the device tile and Take Control view now fit the actual Windows framebuffer as their containers resize; taking control opens the desktop remote alongside Web. · [BUG-0590](../bugs/BUG-0590-2026-10-17-vnc-windows-cropped-and-desktop-controls-hidden.md)
- **Tenancy defense catches `team_id` from anywhere** — the global `enforce_team_scope()` guard now reads `team_id` from the query string, the JSON body, the URL kwargs, **and** the `X-Team-Id` header, and refuses the call when the named team's `tenant_id` is not in the caller's `tenant_ids` claim. Routes that take `team_id` from a path parameter (e.g. `/server/teams/<team_id>`) or from the `X-Team-Id` header were previously covered by the global guard's membership check only; this change adds the tenant-scope check on top. The per-route `@require_team_in_tenant` decorator still exists for explicit use but is no longer required for defense. In plain words: every team-scoped route is now defended against cross-tenant access, including the ones that take `team_id` from anywhere but the query string · [BUG-0586](../bugs/BUG-0586-2026-10-16-require-team-in-tenant-not-applied-to-routes.md) · area: platform
- [BUG-0570](../bugs/BUG-0570-2026-10-16-status-worker-fetch-handler-dead.md) **`vpt-status-worker` no longer ships a dead `fetch` handler that could silently bring back the proxy.** The CF dashboard route binding `virtualpytest.angelstreet.io/*` was removed on 2026-10-16 (Error 1027 was hitting the 100,000 requests/day Free-plan limit because every visitor request ran through the Worker), but the Worker source still carried a full 60+ line proxy plus a 56-line inlined `CUSTOM_ERROR_PAGE` HTML constant and a `renderErrorPage()` helper — none reachable without a route. Removed the `fetch` handler, the HTML constant, and the helper from `infra/cloudflare/status-worker/src/index.ts`; the cron probe (`scheduled` handler, KV writes, Resend DOWN/RECOVERED/DEGRADED emails) is unchanged. `wrangler.toml` route comment rewritten to "DO NOT bind a route — strip fetch handler already prevents a re-bind from silently re-proxying". Without the `fetch` export, an accidental route binding lands on a Worker that returns CF's default 404; the site stays healthy on 1,440 cron invocations/day. The branded "Service temporarily unavailable" page is still served by the separate CF Custom Pages → 500_errors asset (`infra/cloudflare/custom-pages/5xx-error.html`). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. · area: infra
- [BUG-0564](../bugs/BUG-0564-2026-10-16-scope-picker-desktop-all-servers-row-missing.md) **Desktop scope picker was missing the "All servers" row.** TASK-113 PR-C added the row to the mobile `ServerSelector` (rendered through `MobileServerBar` for `isMobile && showsServerPicker`); the desktop `ScopePicker` (rendered through `App.tsx` directly with a 3-column Server/Workspace/Project popover) was missed. ScopePicker now imports `ALL_SERVERS_SENTINEL` + `isAllServersSentinel` from `lib/allServer/scope`, renders the row at the top of the Server column (PublicIcon + bold + Divider), dispatches `handleChange(ALL_SERVERS_SENTINEL)` on click, and is disabled when `availableServers.length === 0`. The closed chip shows "All servers" (not `__all__`) while the sentinel is selected. 4 new Vitest cases in `tests/frontend/allServer/ScopePicker.test.tsx`; the live `/tmp/t113-poc.js` PoC at desktop 1600×1000 now passes. · area: platform
- [BUG-0568](../bugs/BUG-0568-2026-10-16-localstorage-rehydration-drops-all-servers-sentinel.md) **Picking "All servers" then reloading the page silently flipped the picker back to the first configured server.** The rehydration loader in `ServerManagerProvider` accepted only URLs that were in `availableServers[]`; `__all__` is the sentinel value written by the picker but is not in that list, so the loader fell back to `availableServers[0]`. The loader now special-cases the sentinel: `if (saved === ALL_SERVERS_SENTINEL) return ALL_SERVERS_SENTINEL`; real URLs are still validated against `availableServers`; an empty save still falls back to the first configured server. A new Vitest case under `ServerManagerProvider.test.tsx > T16 rehydration` writes `__all__` to `localStorage` and asserts the `selected` testid shows the sentinel verbatim. The live PoC's T16 scenario flips green on the next deploy. · area: platform
- [BUG-0573](../bugs/BUG-0573-2026-10-16-stream-stats-overlay-stale-and-no-disk.md) **The stream-stats overlay's HOST line stopped lying, gained a Disk row, and stopped repeating "behind live".** The overlay's `useHostSnapshot` only fetched on mount, so the HOST line read e.g. `14s old · checked 26s ago` with the two values on unrelated clocks (the "checked" counter grew without bound). The hook now polls every `HOST_SNAPSHOT_POLL_MS = 20_000` ms while mounted, with a `visibilitychange` re-read so a long-idle modal doesn't show stale data when the user looks again. A new `Disk` row (between CPU/RAM and Load) shows `disk_percent` (the FULLEST monitored mount, tooltip names the path — see BUG-0198) plus `R MB/s · W MB/s`; read I/O was silently dropped on the backend, both `get_host_system_stats` and `get_enhanced_system_stats` now publish `disk_read_mb_per_sec` alongside the existing write counter, and `SystemStats` declares the new fields plus the long-unsurfaced `disk_used_gb` / `disk_total_gb` / `disk_worst_mount` / `disk_mounts`. The Latency row drops the `behind live` suffix (the row is already labeled Latency). In plain words: the overlay stays fresh, you can see if the host is actually reading/writing, and the worst mount is one hover away · area: monitoring
- [BUG-0574](../bugs/BUG-0574-2026-10-16-monitoring-overlay-profile-fallback-empty-state.md) **A `counting_cars` device no longer renders the tv_app default overlay.** The bottom-left overlay's branch condition only matched when the JSON's `profile` field was set, so a device configured for any non-`tv_app` profile (`counting_cars`, `counting_eggs`, …) showed the generic FPS / Blackscreen / Freeze / Macroblocks / Audio panel instead of the profile-specific one — with no car counter, no boxes, no KPIs. The condition now uses `effectiveProfile = analysis?.profile || profileName` (where `profileName` comes from `host.system_stats.monitor_profiles[device_id]`, the value the operator picked in device settings), so `ProfileOverlay` lands from the moment the modal opens. The placeholder analysis is synthesised when the monitor hasn't written a JSON yet, and the title falls back to `profileName` so a cold device still shows "counting_cars" instead of a blank. `GenericOverlay` now also surfaces a one-line empty-state hint — "No data yet — check vpt-monitor-img on the host" when the stream is dead, "Monitor running, profile analyzer has not produced data yet" when captures flow but the analyzer hasn't run — so the operator can tell "monitor down" from "monitor running, model still loading" without opening a separate shell. In plain words: the right overlay shows up at the right time, and when nothing is there the panel says so · area: monitoring
- [BUG-0575](../bugs/BUG-0575-2026-10-16-fps-in-should-be-ffmpeg-configured-not-measured.md) **"FPS: in" now shows the rate ffmpeg was *told* to capture at, not the rate we happened to measure.** The overlay used to read `FPS: in —` on a `counting_cars` device with a still-loading model even though ffmpeg was happily running at 5 fps, because the "in" field was the *measured* rate (from capture sequence deltas) — null the moment the monitor had nothing to measure. A new `get_configured_capture_fps()` parses the live ffmpeg process cmdline on the host for `-framerate N` (set by `run_ffmpeg.sh` from `DEVICE${i}_VIDEO_FPS` / `HOST_VIDEO_FPS`) and publishes `host.system_stats.stream_capture_fps[device_id]`. The overlay now uses that as the primary "in" value, and tucks the measured value behind a dim `(≈ N)` suffix when the two differ — so the operator can read "ffmpeg is set to 5 but only 3 are landing per second" at a glance. The tv_app default branch and both `ProfileOverlay` flavours (`UiOverlay`, `GenericOverlay`) get the same treatment; legacy hosts that do not yet publish the new field fall back to the old `fpsIn` behaviour. In plain words: "in" is the configured rate, "≈" is what the host actually observed, and you can tell them apart · area: monitoring
- [BUG-0585](../bugs/BUG-0585-2026-10-16-t113-all-server-edge-glitches.md) **"All servers" edge-glitch bundle: no `wss://__all__/...` spam, Devices renders the fleet, picker hides the row on a single-server deploy.** Three siblings slipped through PR-C because the two-server PoC didn't cover the `availableServers.length <= 1` path. (1) `ServerManagerProvider`'s `/system` socket effect now short-circuits when `selectedServer === ALL_SERVERS_SENTINEL` (or empty), so the browser never attempts `wss://__all__/socket.io/?EIO=4&transport=websocket` and stops logging "WebSocket connection failed" on every reconnect — the page is no longer visibly slow. (2) `HostManagerProvider.allHostsFromServers` now unions every server's hosts in ALL mode instead of running `find(server_url === '__all__')` (which always missed and zeroed the device list); Devices renders the union, with host-keyed device uniqueness preserved and each row's home-server stamp intact for `dispatchForRow`. (3) Both the mobile `ServerSelector` and the desktop `ScopePicker` render the "All servers" row only when `availableServers.length > 1`; one-server deployments have nothing to federate, so the row collapses and the sentinel can never be picked. Two new Vitest cases pin the contract: `__all__` ⇒ `createServerSocket` is **never** called, configured URL ⇒ it's called on `/system`; the `availableServers === []` ScopePicker case is updated from "row disabled" to "row not rendered" and a sibling `availableServers === [one]` case is added. 13/13 across `ServerManagerProvider.test.tsx` + `allServer/ScopePicker.test.tsx` · TASK-113 · area: platform

- [BUG-0583](../bugs/BUG-0583-2026-10-17-host-service-shared-env-overrides-server-url.md) **`vpt-host` on a non-backend host stopped registering when `/opt/virtualpytest/.env` was leaked onto the box.** The `host.service` template loaded `%PROJECT_ROOT%/.env` via `EnvironmentFile=` — that file carries the backend's `SERVER_URL=http://127.0.0.1:5109` and friends. systemd populated the process env from it, then python-dotenv loaded `backend_host/src/.env` with `override=False`, so the systemd value won and the host tried to register with itself (`Connection refused`). On vpt-nipogi the stray `/opt/virtualpytest/.env` arrived by rsync from vpt-pi4; the 24 capture ffmpegs kept running but the host silently dropped off the dashboard. The template now points `EnvironmentFile=` at `%PROJECT_ROOT%/backend_host/src/.env` (the host-specific file) — systemd populates `os.environ` from the same file python-dotenv reads, `override=False` is still respected, and the env stays inspectable via `systemctl show vpt-host`. The legacy `/opt/virtualpytest/.env` on nipogi had `SERVER_NAME/SERVER_URL/SERVER_PORT/SERVER_PUBLIC_URL` commented out as a belt-and-braces guard. Live verification: `vpt-host` re-registered at 10:25 CEST (PID 59686), `getAllHosts` lists `vpt-nipogi` with 25 devices / `online`, `/proc/<pid>/environ` shows `SERVER_URL=http://<host-server>:5109` (RPI4-server). In plain words: a host that ever inherited the backend's `.env` now boots, registers, and shows up on the dashboard without manual surgery · area: monitoring

- [BUG-0587](../bugs/BUG-0587-2026-10-17-apply-max-streams-script-missing-model-and-loop.md) **`scripts/apply_max_streams.py` was writing 6 env vars per device block but skipping the 2 the host needs to populate `device_capabilities.av`** — `DEVICE<N>_MODEL=rtsp_video` and `DEVICE<N>_STREAM_LOOP=1` were missing from both the REPLACE and APPEND branches, so 8 of the 12 NEW devices on vpt-nipogi registered with `av=None` and the `/device-control` page silently filtered them out (operator saw 17 of 25 nipogi tiles). The fix: the script now emits all 8 required vars (`NAME`, `MODEL`, `STREAM_TYPE`, `STREAM_LOOP`, `VIDEO`, `VIDEO_FPS`, `VIDEO_CAPTURE_PATH`, `VIDEO_STREAM_PATH`) on both branches, and adds a "Pass 2.5" that PATCHes any already-present device missing the 3 capability lines (so re-running the script on a host with broken blocks recovers them). A new pytest at `tests/scripts/test_apply_max_streams_av_block.py` exercises the REPLACE, PATCH, and APPEND paths against an in-memory `.env` fixture and asserts every block has the full 8-var set (1/1 green). Live verification on nipogi (2026-10-17): the 8 missing devices (FR Kids 1/2/3, FR Travel, FR Comedy, FR Series, FR Regional 3/4) now show on the dashboard, `Device 30 → 38`, the `tv_lite` filter chip `21 → 29`, page 2 of `/device-control` lists the 6 previously-hidden nipogi tiles next to the off-host devices. In plain words: `apply_max_streams.py` no longer hides devices from the dashboard · [tests/scripts/test_apply_max_streams_av_block.py](../../tests/scripts/test_apply_max_streams_av_block.py) · area: monitoring

- **A black picture no longer shows as live** — when the box is black, freeze (and therefore motion) is skipped, so Localize kept the last live-TV motion and the tile badge stayed on `live`. Blackscreen / no-signal now win: the badge is `Black Screen · 100 %`. In plain words: a dead HDMI no longer looks like a live channel · [BUG-0567](../bugs/BUG-0567-2026-10-16-live-badge-on-blackscreen.md)
- **`NavigationExecutor.load_navigation_tree` exists again (BUG-0594)** — `backend_host/src/services/navigation/navigation_executor.py` line 1222 had a stray top-level `def _now_iso()` at indent 0 right in the middle of the `NavigationExecutor` class body. A top-level `def` inside a class closes the class early in Python, so every method declared after it (`load_navigation_tree` at line 3416, `get_navigation_preview` at line 3294, `get_execution_status` at line 3257, plus the verification-recording block) became a module-level function, not a `NavigationExecutor` method. `hasattr(NavigationExecutor, 'load_navigation_tree')` was `False` despite the `def` being visible in the source. Every host script that calls `device.navigation_executor.load_navigation_tree(...)` (the `pokemon-blue-web` script, several smoke scripts including `smoke_video_stream` / `smoke_heatmap` / `smoke_network_server`) hit `AttributeError: 'NavigationExecutor' object has no attribute 'load_navigation_tree'` on every run since this regression was added — the saffron GUIDE-skip fix from BUG-0589 was already deployed to host-clone-1, but the host runs had been failing in 5 s with this traceback instead of ever reaching the saffron step. The fix promotes `_now_iso()` to a `@staticmethod` inside the class (its original intent; no `self` arg needed at module level either), which puts every later `def` back where Python expects them. Post-deploy: `pokemon-long-play-jev` boots the runner, the saffron GUIDE-skip fires (`guide_skip, from_=9, to=11, map=6`), and the runner advances · [BUG-0594](../bugs/BUG-0594-2026-10-17-navigation-executor-class-closed-by-stray-module-func.md) · area: platform

- **TASK-33 saffron GUIDE-skip also resets the host's stale `same_spot` counter (BUG-0595)** — once the saffron GUIDE bump (BUG-0589) lands on a host whose memo already carried `stall.spot_ticks = 600` from previous saffron-replay fails, the next `overworld_tick` saw the runner still at `(39, 20)` (the bump doesn't move the player on screen), tick the counter to 601, and re-stall even though the runner's plan had just changed to the saffron-area edge. `_saffron_skip_drink_detour` now zeroes `stall.spot` / `stall.spot_ticks` / `stall.progress_s` whenever it advances GUIDE, so the next observe() starts a fresh 600-tick budget — long enough to walk from `(39, 20)` east to the saffron-area edges `(49, 10)` / `(49, 11)` and step onto Route 7. 2 new tests in `tests/shared/test_pokemon.py` (reset on bump / no-reset when no bump). 109/109 pokemon tests pass · [BUG-0595](../bugs/BUG-0595-2026-10-17-saffron-guide-skip-must-reset-stall-counters.md) · area: task-33

- **TASK-33 saffron no longer stalls at Celadon `(39, 20)` after the drink is in the bag** — the previous saffron fix (`47650221e`) walked the runner to a saffron-edge square when its part of the map lacked one, but with the drink already bought the runner's `GUIDE` pointer was still parked on the first `CELADON` entry of the saffron path, so `overworld_tick` asked `go_to_map` to route it back to `MART_ROOF` (a redundant drink-buy trip). At `(39, 20)` every UP press is the south-gate warp `(39, 19)` (already in `bad_warps`), so the runner spun in place: 329 walks / 600 ticks → `same_spot` stall every saffron instance replay (`20261015-091504`, `-121504`, `-124503`). `runner.saffron_step` now bumps `GUIDE` past `MART_ROOF` to the second `CELADON` entry when the runner is on Celadon with the drink in the bag, so the route uses the saffron-area edge instead of routing back to the vending-machine roof. One `guide_skip` event is logged so the stall detector / review scripts notice the path change. 4 new tests in `tests/shared/test_pokemon.py` (GUIDE-on-first-CELADON bumps, off-Celadon doesn't, drink-not-bought doesn't, idempotent on second-CELADON). 107/107 pokemon tests pass · [BUG-0589](../bugs/BUG-0589-2026-10-17-pokemon-saffron-celadon-guide-detour-stall.md) · area: task-33
- **Take Control Monitoring no longer hides the Localize chip** — turning Monitoring on unmounted the top-right screen name (`home · 96 %`) and sat the ERROR badge on the same corner. The chip stays up; ERROR/WARNING sit just below it. In plain words: you still see which screen it is while Monitoring is open · [BUG-0566](../bugs/BUG-0566-2026-10-16-monitoring-hides-localize-badge.md) · area: monitoring
- **Cloudflare status worker now renders the branded 5xx page on the real domain** — the dashboard route for `vpt-status-worker` was bound to the typo `<app-host>/*` (missing "py"), and the Worker never fired on traffic to `virtualpytest.angelstreet.io`. Users saw Cloudflare's generic "Connection timed out / Error 522" instead of the branded "VirtualPyTest — Service temporarily unavailable" card. The cron / email side was healthy: every minute the probe marked the origin down and Resend delivered the `[VirtualPyTest] origin DOWN — 2 consecutive failures` email to the operator — only the fetch handler had no real traffic to render on. Fixed by PATCH-ing the route pattern via the CF API (`PUT /zones/<zone>/workers/routes/<route>` → `pattern: "virtualpytest.angelstreet.io/*"`) and updating the typo + adding a copy-paste warning in `wrangler.toml` and the worker README. In plain words: when the origin is down, visitors now see the VirtualPyTest card with a Retry button, not Cloudflare's default page · [BUG-0554](../bugs/BUG-0554-2026-10-16-status-worker-route-typo-branded-page-never-shows.md) · area: infra
- **REC preview card header is slimmed down to its essential slots** — every preview tile's header used to read `stb4 - vpt-pi1 [tags] [localize] [AVQ] [status] ⭐`; the device-flags chip, the `LocalizeButton`, the AVQ feature link, the lock indicator (when a script is running) and the `DeviceStatusChip` are gone. The header now carries only the `displayName` + maintenance badge on the left and the favorite star on the right (with the edit-mode checkbox between them when relevant). The full lock / script state still appears in the `RunningScriptNameBadge` in the card body, so no information is lost — only the redundant header chrome is. In plain words: the preview tiles breathe, and the device name is never squeezed off the right · [BUG-0553](../bugs/BUG-0553-2026-10-15-rec-preview-header-slimmed.md) · area: device-control
- **Take Control screenshot works again** — the camera and AI-query buttons in the stream modal failed with "video segment missing" whenever the modal reused the tile's stream (since 2026-10-11). They now take the latest frame from the host when the player reports no segment or the stream is WebRTC, in about 0.2 s, and each click shows the current frame instead of a cached one. In plain words: the screenshot button no longer fails on a playing stream · [BUG-0552](../bugs/BUG-0552-2026-10-15-rec-modal-screenshot-segment-missing.md) · area: device-control
- **Take Control / stream modal header shows the same title as the preview tile** — the modal header rendered only `device.device_name` (`stbv1+`), while the preview tile under it rendered `displayName = "stbv1+ - vpt-mf1"`; two different titles for the same `host + device` pair. `RecStreamModalHeader` now mirrors `RecHostPreview`'s `displayName` exactly (VNC devices show only `host.host_name`, every other device shows `"<device_name> - <host_name>"`), and the `_Host$`-suffix strip is dropped for parity with the preview. In plain words: opening Take Control no longer renames the device · [BUG-0551](../bugs/BUG-0551-2026-10-15-rec-modal-header-shown-only-target-name.md) · area: device-control
- **Newly created Project always counted 0 members, even though the creator should be the first owner** — `POST /server/projects` was passing `request.user_id` (a `profiles.id`) as `created_by_team_member_id` in the `projects_members` insert; the FK targets `team_members.id`, so the insert raised and was silently swallowed. The route now resolves `_caller_team_member_ids()[0]` before calling, and `projects_db.create_project` skips the insert for a falsy id defensively. Every new project ships with its creator as owner. In plain words: a project created on `/projects` shows the creator under Owners · [BUG-0547](../bugs/BUG-0547-2026-10-15-projects-creator-fk-leaves-zero-members.md)
- **Project card always read "1 tags" because the project's auto-created scope tag was counted as a user tag** — every project auto-creates its scope tag in `projects_tags`, and `_enrich` returned every linked tag as one `tag_ids` list; the card's chip was reading that count. New `projects_tags.is_scope BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE` (migration `20261015b_projects_tags_is_scope.sql`) marks the auto-link, and the enriched project now exposes `scope_tag` + `user_tag_count` so the UI can count only user-attached tags. Doc claims "the slug is hidden" — it now is. In plain words: a project with no tags reads 0 · [BUG-0548](../bugs/BUG-0548-2026-10-15-projects-card-shows-scope-tag-count.md)
- **The Target stream on a Windows / VNC host fills the AI-Agent panel** — the iframe used a hard-coded `380×180` target and never re-measured the wrapper, so widening the Target panel left a `306×180` sliver in the top-left of an otherwise empty container. The Target panel now hosts a ResizeObserver-driven measured-area box and the iframe is scaled against it (same pattern as the Rec page). In plain words: a Windows or VNC target now uses the whole Target panel, no manual resize needed · [BUG-0546](../bugs/BUG-0546-2026-10-15-ai-agent-vnc-target-stream-too-narrow.md) · area: device-control
- **Video card click did not transition to the iframe** — Atlas's chat video card rendered the poster + play button, the click handler fired (`setPlaying(true)` ran), but the ReactMarkdown subtree re-mounted the `<VideoCard>` on every parent re-render, so the local `useState<boolean>(false)` reset to `false` before the conditional re-evaluated and the `<iframe>` branch was never reached. Pulling the `<VideoCard>` out of the ReactMarkdown tree (split the content at every `[[video:<id>]]` token, render text through ReactMarkdown, render video segments as direct React siblings) gives the card a stable position in the React tree, so `setPlaying(true)` now actually flips the conditional and the YouTube embed loads on click · [BUG-0549](../bugs/BUG-0549-2026-10-15-video-card-click-state-lost-on-rerender.md) · area: ai
- **A fresh VPT host installed on a Debian 13 desktop image auto-suspends via GNOME and goes offline ~10 min after boot** — `install_host.sh` does not strip the `task-desktop` metapackage, so `gdm3` + `gnome-shell` + `gsd-power` ship on the box. With no user driving the desktop, the GNOME idle-suspend policy fires (kernel ring shows `ACPI: PM: Preparing to enter system sleep state S3` plus `ata1.00: Entering standby power mode` in the same second, journal shows `systemd-sleep: Performing sleep operation 'suspend'…`). On a server with no `wake_source` and an outbound-only heartbeat, the box disappears from the UI and the SSH path drops in lockstep. Fix: `apt-get remove --purge` the desktop stack (`task-desktop` `task-gnome-desktop` `gdm3` `gnome-shell` `gnome-session` `gnome-control-center` `gnome-software` `gnome-terminal` `gnome-tweaks` plus apps like `nautilus` `evolution` `rhythmbox` `cheese` …) and `apt autoremove --purge`, then `systemctl mask suspend.target hibernate.target hybrid-sleep.target suspend-then-hibernate.target` as defence in depth, and add `HandleLidSwitch=ignore` / `IdleAction=ignore` to `/etc/systemd/logind.conf`. In plain words: a VPT host installed on a Debian 13 desktop image now stays online the same way `vpt-pi4` and `vpt-nipogi` do · [BUG-0550](../bugs/BUG-0550-2026-10-15-lenovo1-gdm-gnome-suspend.md) · area: infra
- **Roll out is offered on every build, not only those with manual steps** — on Release Notes, the Platform update button under a build disappeared when that build needed no manual step, so an admin on a newer install could not open the update for an older build. The button now shows for admins on every build. In plain words: you can always pick an older build to roll back to
- **Platform update opened from Release Notes shows the build you chose** — `?to=<build>` was only resolved after an update run had started, so the header read `installed → —`; the page now loads the core release tags on open, shows the requested build at once, and flags a build with no release tag on the core remote as "not published" (step 2 preselects nothing; Roll out in Compare is disabled for it). In plain words: the Roll out link now tells you which build you are about to install, and warns you when it cannot be installed from GitHub · [BUG-0544](../bugs/BUG-0544-2026-10-15-platform-update-to-param-shows-dash.md)
- **The on-request database backup runs outside the VM agent's sandbox** — the agent started `vpt-db-backup.sh` as a child process, which inherited the agent's read-only filesystem and failed with `open /data/backups/…dump: read-only file system`; it now starts the one-shot unit `vpt-db-backup.service` through systemd (the agent stays sandboxed, new setting `VM_AGENT_DB_BACKUP_UNIT` replaces `VM_AGENT_DB_BACKUP_CMD`), and `install.sh --action db-backup` installs the unit. In plain words: "Backup on request" in the Platform update can write the backup again · [BUG-0543](../bugs/BUG-0543-2026-10-15-db-backup-action-read-only-filesystem.md)
- **The caption playback player shows the right video from any timezone** — the stream modal of a `caption_playback` device took the archive hour from the browser's clock, so a viewer outside the host's timezone watched video two hours old under a correct-looking "2:33 behind live" badge; it now reads the host's clock from the archive manifest and waits for it before playing. In plain words: the delayed picture is the one from 2½ minutes ago wherever you are · [BUG-0542](../bugs/BUG-0542-2026-10-15-caption-player-uses-browser-clock.md)
- **Atlas chat console no longer floods with `[ChatMessages] Render check:` lines while a turn runs** — two debug `console.log` calls lived in the render bodies of `ChatMessages` and `AgentChat`, so each of the 4 renders/sec driven by the live-elapsed-counters timer produced one log; both are removed, the 250 ms timer itself stays (it is what makes the `mm:ss.t` chips tick). In plain words: the Chat devtools console stays quiet during an Atlas turn · [BUG-0541](../bugs/BUG-0541-2026-10-15-agent-chat-render-log-storm.md)
- **Host admin endpoints are no longer reachable through the server auto-proxy** — `/server/system/*` paths without a dedicated server route (run command, update core, apply bundle, update prerequisites) were forwarded to the host for any signed-in non-admin user; auto-proxy now refuses every `system/` path except the VNC password lookup. In plain words: only admins can run host maintenance actions again · [BUG-0540](../bugs/BUG-0540-2026-10-15-auto-proxy-exposes-host-admin-endpoints.md)
- **Docs links with a #section open at that section** — the docs viewer renders the guide after the browser's one-time hash scroll, so `…/platform-update#step-0` landed at the top; it now scrolls to the anchor once the page is rendered and on hash changes. In plain words: "How this step works" opens the guide at that step · [BUG-0538](../bugs/BUG-0538-2026-10-15-docs-deep-link-ignores-anchor.md)
- **Agent Chat assistant text wraps inside a centered 820 px column** — the chat column used to span the whole panel, so a multi-paragraph answer wrapped at 150–200 chars per line and was noticeably harder to read than ChatGPT or Claude; the chat stream now caps the column at 820 px (≈ 80 chars per line, like Claude) and centers it, the assistant body rises from 0.82 rem / 1.55 to 0.95 rem / 1.65 so headings still feel hierarchical, the streaming reasoning text gets the same treatment, and the user bubble shrinks to fit short questions instead of stretching to the full chat width. In plain words: the AI chat reads like ChatGPT/Claude now · [BUG-0539](../bugs/BUG-0539-2026-10-15-agent-chat-assistant-text-too-wide.md)
- **Agent Chat Target panel cannot be dragged below 240 px** — the resize `useEffect` shared one `MIN = 180` across all three side panels; dragging the divider next to **Target** could stop at 180 px, collapsing the 9:18 mobile stream into a vertical strip with unreadable icons and weather text. The device panel now has its own 240 px floor (a phone preview needs ~220 px after padding); history and remote keep the 180 px generic minimum because they only carry text. In plain words: the phone tile stays readable when you make room for the chat · [BUG-0537](../bugs/BUG-0537-2026-10-15-agent-chat-target-panel-resize-too-narrow.md)
- **The overlay detector calls a promo slate a notification or a popup** — a bright picture reaching the top-right corner was read as a notification banner whatever its size, and one spanning the frame height as a popup; a notification is now a band (≤ 45 % wide, ≤ 30 % high) and a popup must float inside the frame. In plain words: Localize and the monitor no longer see a banner on a screensaver · [BUG-0535](../bugs/BUG-0535-2026-10-14-overlay-calls-a-promo-slate-a-notification.md)
- **Pokémon Blue no longer loops on the Lift Key** — the runner gave up on the Rocket guard after 3 talks and then poked the still-hidden key ball for ever (stuck since 5 Oct); it now talks to him until the ball shows, then takes it · [BUG-0534](../bugs/BUG-0534-2026-10-14-pokemon-lift-key-stall.md)
- **Localize says "Live video" instead of "No match" when the screen is playing video** — an abstain with full-screen motion now reads "Live video" in both popover steps · [BUG-0533](../bugs/BUG-0533-2026-10-14-localize-live-video-says-no-match.md)
- **Settings dropdown: bug icon on Status, "Open a ticket" has none** — the Status row's beetle icon (MUI `BugReport`) belonged on the row that opens the ticket dialog; Status now wears the same lightning bolt already used on the live-stream header, and **Open a ticket** gets the bug. In plain words: the icons match what the rows do · [BUG-0536](../bugs/BUG-0536-2026-10-15-settings-menu-icon-misassigned.md)
- **Heatmap respects AI-triage discards** — a tile whose freeze/black-screen/audio alert was discarded (by triage or by you) turns green again and no longer counts in Incidents, for any past minute; tiles with no alert yet or a kept alert stay red. Under OK/KO the full mosaic is shown with non-matching tiles dimmed. In plain words: the heatmap no longer cries wolf about incidents the AI already judged harmless · [BUG-0531](../bugs/BUG-0531-2026-10-14-heatmap-ignores-ai-triage-discards.md)
- **Go to node in Take Control is a compact view** — the overlay now shows the path as a breadcrumb with step count and ETA, the Verify choice and Run on one row, and the steps behind a Show steps toggle (one line per step, no retry, failure or wait columns that got clipped). In plain words: the panel fits over the video again
- **The Bluetooth remote's "Waking STB…" pill is gone; the header dot blinks instead** — the floating amber pill at the top-left of the remote image, redundant with the disabled "Waking…" Power button and the steady amber dot in the panel header, is removed. `RemotePanel` now plays a 1.1 s opacity pulse (1 ↔ 0.35) on the 10×10 header dot while the link is being woken or recovered, and keeps the dot still for non-transient amber states (e.g. connected but HID missing). The heading + subtext and the disabled Power button still own the human-readable status and the clickable progress; the dot is the motion cue. In plain words: one indicator, in one place, that breathes while a wake is in flight · [BUG-0528](../bugs/BUG-0528-2026-10-14-ble-remote-waking-badge-redundant-with-blinking-dot.md)
- **Run Tests queues busy devices instead of blocking Run** — a device already running a test stays selectable; Run starts the free ones and queues the busy ones until they free up. In plain words: pick any device and press Run, busy ones wait their turn · [BUG-0527](../bugs/BUG-0527-2026-10-14-run-tests-busy-target-blocks-run.md)
- **Failure report links open instead of showing 401** — "View target report" and "View Debug Report" after a failed navigation opened a page the proxy refused without a session; they now open it through the host session like screenshots do · [BUG-0532](../bugs/BUG-0532-2026-10-14-report-links-401-unauthorized.md). In plain words: clicking a failure report shows the report, not Unauthorized
- **Shared report links open the Timeline picture; Shared report links keep their screenshots and video** — the Timeline link in a shared snapshot pointed at a page snapshots cannot carry and showed "not included"; it now opens the timeline image Also: a busy storage proxy could refuse the snapshot's file checks, so the link opened with every image and the video removed; the check is now retried and a snapshot with no files at all fails instead of being published · [BUG-0530](../bugs/BUG-0530-2026-10-14-shared-snapshot-without-images-or-video.md). In plain words: the Chronologie link works in shared reports. In plain words: share the report again and the pictures are there
- **Go-to panel no longer shows a green success box while still navigating** — the progress message ("Navigating to home...") appeared in a green box with a check icon before the run finished. It now shows as a neutral running box with a running icon, turns green only when done, and all alerts use the app's fixed success / error / warning colours · [BUG-0529](../bugs/BUG-0529-2026-10-14-running-shown-as-success.md). In plain words: green now always means finished
- **Go to node: Run can be pressed again right after a run, and failures are readable** — after a passed or failed run the Run button stayed disabled (a leftover error, or "already at destination", blocked it) and the failure box showed red text on red in a large font. Run now stays enabled and clears the previous result when started; the Take Control panel shows status as a compact outlined box with small report links, and the panel is about 70 percent size · [BUG-0526](../bugs/BUG-0526-2026-10-14-goto-run-stays-disabled-after-run.md). In plain words: you can re-run a navigation straight away and actually read why it failed
- **The floating Ask AI button no longer sits on top of the footer** — on long pages scrolled to the bottom, the purple "Ask AI" Fab (bottom-left, `Cmd+K`) used to land on top of the "© 202…" footer text because the Fab's `bottom: 24` was smaller than the in-flow Footer's own height. Lifted to `bottom: 80` (the Fab is `size="medium"`, ≈ 56 px) so the button keeps its ≈ 24 px visual offset, now measured from the footer's top edge instead of the viewport edge; mobile/tablet already hide the Fab, so only desktop is affected · [BUG-0525](../bugs/BUG-0525-2026-10-14-ask-ai-fab-overlays-footer.md). In plain words: the copyright line is fully readable and the button is still one tap away
- **Device Info opens on the first device instead of an empty dropdown** — `/device-info` without a `?device_name=&host_name=` deep link used to render an empty "Device" picker with no hint, so the Device / Gateway tabs and the override table only showed up once you manually picked something. The page now auto-selects the first device (favorite first, then alphabetical) once the host list has loaded; a deep link whose target is out of scope still surfaces as an empty dropdown so a stale link does not silently re-point at another device, and the existing "No devices with info" placeholder is unchanged when nothing has keys · [BUG-0524](../bugs/BUG-0524-2026-10-14-device-info-opens-with-empty-dropdown.md). In plain words: the page lands directly on a device instead of asking you to pick one
- **The Bluetooth remote no longer disappears when the link drops** — once a bonded STB has worked in the session, a lost BLE link (supervision timeout on a busy onboard radio, e.g. vpt-pi1) keeps the buttons on screen with an amber "Reconnecting…" badge ("Waking STB…" while the background wake runs), with no time limit; the next key press reconnects in ~2 s. The wake/pairing screen only shows with no bond, the daemon down, while pairing, or after pressing Power · [BUG-0523](../bugs/BUG-0523-2026-10-14-ble-remote-unmounts-on-short-link-drop.md). In plain words: the remote stays on screen through drops; just press a key
- **Platform update prerequisites: the frontend is no longer skipped, the GitHub check works, and the sudoers rule no longer hands out root** — three defects found running the checks on the lab. (1) `FRONTEND_DEPLOY_HOST` is now explicit and read the same way by the check, the pull deploy and the Code deployment buttons: a host or IP = a separate frontend VM over SSH, `local` = the frontend runs on the server VM (built and `vpt-frontend` restarted after the server tree is updated), `none` = no frontend, not set = the check is KO "Set FRONTEND_DEPLOY_HOST (host, local or none)" and the deploy is refused instead of silently leaving the frontend old; the hardcoded lab IP default is removed. (2) The GitHub check passed the token as a Bearer header in the command line (visible in `ps`, and GitHub wants Basic): the check, the bundle builder's core and overlay clones and its candidate listing now give git the token through a temporary askpass file (user `x-access-token`, `VPT_GITHUB_TOKEN`, else `GITHUB_TOKEN`), never argv or a URL, only to github.com. (3) The drop-in granted root on `reconcile_feature_units.sh` inside the service user's own tree: it now grants a root-owned copy, `/usr/local/sbin/vpt-reconcile-feature-units`, installed by the new `setup/sudoers/install_vpt_update_sudoers.sh` (visudo-validated), and the check says "installed reconcile helper is older than the tree" when a newer copy ships. The frontend VM's `deploy` account keeps `sudo -u vpt_user` for any command by design (the guide explains why a narrower rule would be theatre). In plain words: the update now tells you when it does not know where the frontend is, the GitHub check no longer leaks the token and passes with a valid one, and the service account can no longer edit its way to root through the update. · [BUG-0522](../bugs/BUG-0522-2026-10-14-update-prerequisites-lab-run-frontend-github-sudoers.md)
- **A change to a feature's frontend now actually reaches the site** — the freshness stamp that decides whether the 1m45s frontend build runs hashed `frontend/` but not `features/`, so a change confined to GitLab, Zammad, Tuya, CICD, AVQ, the drone or QuickTest left the stamp untouched: the deploy printed `dist is current — skipping vite build`, ended with `OK` and `Done.`, and served the previous bundle. Nothing in the output said so — the rsync does land the sources, so the files on the machine look correct, and the deployed git HEAD is stale anyway because rsync copies files without moving history. The stamp now covers each feature's `frontend/` tree (the files vite imports, not its `lib/` and `db/`, so a python edit still costs no rebuild) and the `VITE_DISABLED_FEATURES` switch, which arrives as an environment variable rather than a file and so can turn a feature on or off without anything on disk moving. Measured cost: +0.2 s per stamp, against a 90 s build. Until this reaches the servers, a feature deploy needs `VPT_FORCE_BUILD=1` run on the frontend VM, and after any deploy the honest check is grepping `dist/assets/*.js` for your own code · [BUG-0521](../bugs/BUG-0521-2026-10-14-feature-change-deploys-as-a-no-op.md). In plain words: a fix in a feature now shows up on the site instead of quietly staying on the server
- **Control is disabled when a device has no remote** — on a device whose host registered no remote controller, the REC stream modal still offered Control: taking it locked the device and auto-opened the remote panel onto "No remote configured", with nothing to press and the lock still held. The button is now greyed out and says why in its tooltip, and the action is blocked too, so the device is never locked for a panel that cannot work. VNC desktops are deliberately untouched — they are driven over VNC, not by a remote, and gating them on one would have disabled Control on every `host_vnc` device · [BUG-0520](../bugs/BUG-0520-2026-10-14-rec-control-enabled-without-a-remote.md). In plain words: if the remote is not there, the button tells you before you click instead of after
- **The customer bundle keeps its changelog and delivers its own archive** — `customer_bundle.py build --prev-pin <tag>` fetched the previous pin together with a branch of the same name, which git refuses when the pin is only a tag, so the delivery note silently lost its changelog; it now fetches the tag or the branch it really is and fails loudly if the pin does not exist. The tool also picked "the newest archive" from a shared output folder, so a build could report an earlier customer's or version's bundle; it now collects exactly the archive the builder announced. The GitLab token file is deleted after the overlay is cloned, `candidates` reports an unreachable core remote instead of an empty list, and `--gitlab-url` (or `$VPT_GITLAB_URL`) is one address for both the API and the clone. In plain words: the delivery note has its changelog, and the bundle you are told about is the one that was just built · [BUG-0518](../bugs/BUG-0518-2026-10-13-customer-bundle-previous-pin-and-stale-archive.md)
- **Tello tile: a drone that is switched off no longer looks connected** — the Device tile kept the drone icon, "Starting video…", a green dot and the last battery/temperature after the drone was powered off or the host's Wi-Fi had left it, and offered no Scan chip. The host now reports the drone as connected only while its Wi-Fi is on the drone and its state stream is fresh; otherwise the tile shows "Drone not found", a grey dot and the Scan chip, and the old battery reading disappears · [BUG-0519](../bugs/BUG-0519-2026-10-13-tello-tile-stale-after-power-off.md). In plain words: switch the drone off and the tile tells you so; switch it on and press Scan to pair it again
- **A host takes more than 30 devices** — the stream start-up, the capture-folder, permission and RAM-disk setup, the device config loader and the Docker entrypoint all stopped at DEVICE30, so DEVICE31 and up got no stream and no folder. They now look up to DEVICE99 (override with `MAX_DEVICES`). In plain words: a host can run 40+ streams
- **Tello: Take off comes back after landing** — a Land sent to a drone already on the floor used to answer "Land: error" and leave the remote believing the drone was still flying, so Take off stayed unavailable. It now says "Already on the ground" and the button returns to Take off; a land with the drone really in the air still reports a failure · [BUG-0517](../bugs/BUG-0517-2026-10-13-tello-land-button-disabled-after-landing.md). In plain words: if the drone is down, tapping Land is harmless and you can take off again straight away
- **No more AI calls that cannot succeed** — an AI account without credit gets one test call an hour instead of one per process and per CI browser task (browser-use stops at the first refusal and the CI smoke is refused before it starts); Settings tests and agent chats skip a paused model; the MiniMax VLM no longer retries or receives a flat frame it answers with nothing; an unreachable LM Studio is not called again for a minute · [BUG-0514](../bugs/BUG-0514-2026-10-13-ai-refused-calls-still-leave-after-pause.md) · [BUG-0515](../bugs/BUG-0515-2026-10-13-vlm-empty-answers-on-blank-frames.md) · [BUG-0516](../bugs/BUG-0516-2026-10-13-ai-unreachable-endpoint-called-every-time.md). In plain words: the AI usage page's "wasted" number drops to the unavoidable test calls
- **A wrong endpoint no longer says "host_name required"** — any `/server/...` address that matched no route fell into the catch-all, which proxies to a device and answered `400 host_name required`. So a caller that simply had the path wrong was told it was missing a *device host* parameter, which sent the investigation after the host layer instead of the one line that needed changing (it is why the empty GitLab repository below was so hard to trace). The catch-all now names the path that matched no route, keeps both possible readings in the message, adds `unmatched_endpoint` and `auto_proxy_fallback` so a caller can check without reading prose, and writes a `⚠️ UNMATCHED ROUTE` line to the server log instead of a line that reads like an ordinary device call. Real device calls, successes and every other error are exactly as before. A fresh server install also gains `zip`, so packaging a delivery can produce a zip and not only a tar.gz. In plain words: a mistyped endpoint tells you it is mistyped, in the page and in the logs, and a new server can build a zip · [BUG-0513](../bugs/BUG-0513-unmatched-endpoint-blamed-a-device-host.md)
- **Joining or scanning for a Tello no longer freezes the host** — the pairing route's Wi-Fi join (up to 30 s of `nmcli`), the drone scan and the status poll's Wi-Fi check ran `nmcli` on the request itself, which stops every other request on a vpt-host. They now run on an OS thread like the flight commands. In plain words: the other tiles keep playing while a drone is being paired · [BUG-0512](../bugs/BUG-0512-2026-10-13-tello-pair-wifi-join-freezes-host.md)
- **Flying or pairing a Tello no longer freezes the host** — a take-off, land, move or the pairing link waited for the drone on the request itself (up to 40 s for a take-off with no answer), and vpt-host serves on gevent without monkey patching, so every other request on that host — stream playlists included — stopped until it gave up. The wait now runs on an OS thread while the request waits cooperatively; battery reads never wait behind a command, and a joystick frame behind a take-off is dropped instead of queued. In plain words: the other tiles keep playing while the drone is taking off · [BUG-0511](../bugs/BUG-0511-2026-10-13-tello-flight-command-freezes-host.md)
- **The AI gateway passthrough respects the no-credit pause** — the browser agent kept calling a provider with no credit (160 refused calls overnight); now the server answers 402 itself while the pause lasts and counts one skipped call. [BUG-0510](../bugs/BUG-0510-2026-10-13-ai-passthrough-ignores-no-credit-pause.md). In plain words: no more failing calls to a provider that is out of credit.
- **Browser-agent AI calls say who made them** — the host sends its caller with every call and the server stores a client hash for a client that names nobody, so "Unknown API client" can be traced. [BUG-0509](../bugs/BUG-0509-2026-10-13-ai-passthrough-calls-unattributed.md). In plain words: every call on the AI usage page has an owner.
- **GitLab projects show their files and README again** — a connected GitLab listed its projects and its tiles, but opening one gave an empty file tree, no README and a click that did nothing. Three separate faults sat on top of each other. Every GitLab project path is `group/project`, and the routes took the project as a path segment; a path segment cannot hold an encoded slash, so no route matched and the request fell through to a catch-all that answered `400 host_name required` — a *device host* parameter this feature has never used, which is why the error pointed somewhere unrelated. GitLab also has no "give me the README" endpoint at all (it answers 404), so the README is now found by name in the project root and read like any other file. And the file list and the README were fetched together in one `Promise.all`, so the README failing also emptied the file list — a repository with no README looked like an empty repository. It only showed up once an instance was connected to a real server, because the bundler clones through git and never used these routes, and the route test accepted the failing `400`. In plain words: pick a project and you see its files and README like GitLab does · [BUG-0507](../bugs/BUG-0507-gitlab-namespaced-project-matched-no-route.md)
- **An all-black screen is reported as a blackscreen, and an unplugged HDMI cable as a no-signal** — the Alerts page grouped an all-black HDMI capture under `freeze`, and there was no `no signal` alert at all: SMPTE colour bars were either swallowed silently or rolled into a freeze. The detector itself now enforces the priority (a frame already tagged `blackscreen` or `no_signal` cannot be tagged `freeze` on the same pass), the blackscreen pixel threshold sits at 80% (was 95%) so a real no-input capture whose JPG has a handful of bright pixels stops slipping through, `no_signal` is a first-class incident type alongside `blackscreen`, and the chip on the Alerts table says "no signal" in plain words. In plain words: the right fault name shows up, and the freeze bucket only carries real freezes · [BUG-0508](../bugs/BUG-0508-2026-10-13-blackscreen-no-signal-must-be-priority-over-freeze.md)
- **Tello take-off no longer times out on a paired drone** — the remote panel failed with "timeout after 10.0s waiting for reply to 'command'" while the drone's video was playing. A Tello only answers the address:port that sent its first `command`; pairing checked the link from its own socket, then the controller talked from another random port the drone ignored. Every command now leaves from one fixed port (8889), pairing opens the controller's own link instead of a separate one, the link stays open after landing, and a drone that was switched off and on again gets `command` again before the next move. Reading the drone's battery no longer freezes the host: with the drone off, each 2 s telemetry poll used to block vpt-host for 10 s (it serves on gevent without monkey patching), so every stream playlist on that host hung until Cloudflare returned 524; reads now answer at once and link in the background, retrying at most every 10 s. In plain words: the flight buttons talk to the drone from the one address it listens to · [BUG-0506](../bugs/BUG-0506-2026-10-13-tello-takeoff-timeout-wrong-source-port.md)
- **`supervisorctl status` no longer reports `adb` as FATAL on Docker hosts** — every host container logged four `adb` spawn/exit cycles in 7 s and ended with `adb entered FATAL state, too many start retries too quickly`, next to a perfectly healthy `✅ Registered ADB verification`. The block ran `adb start-server`, which daemonizes: it forks the real server into the background and the parent exits 0 straight away, which supervisord read as an unexpected exit. adb was never broken — the server supervisord gave up on was still running as an unsupervised orphan. Supervisord now supervises the adb server itself in the foreground (`adb -L tcp:5037 nodaemon server`), on the same `tcp:5037` endpoint as before, so `supervisorctl status adb` reports `RUNNING` and a crashed server is restarted instead of abandoned. Android hosts are unaffected: `adb -s ip:port` and `adb connect` work against the supervised server, and a client still auto-starts a server when none is listening. In plain words: the scary FATAL on every Docker host is gone, and adb is actually supervised · [BUG-0505](../bugs/BUG-0505-2026-10-13-adb-supervisord-fatal-daemonizing-start-server.md)
- **The image pipeline published nothing for 16 days** — no GHCR image for the server, host, frontend, minio or minio-mc had been built since `main-2026.09.27-9394`, because the standalone-bundle job failed at its "every mount resolves inside the bundle" check and the image jobs were skipped with it. Two bind mounts declared in `docker-compose.yml` had never been reconciled with the bundle: `frontend/public/docs` (added 2026-09-26, the docs tree the web app serves at `/docs`) was not copied into the archive at all, and `/dev/null` (added 2026-10-05, hiding Grafana's dashboard template from file provisioning) sat outside the check's `/dev` host-hardware exemption. The bundle now ships `frontend/public/docs` — the archive grows from 0.9 MB to 6.0 MB, still ~50× smaller than the clone it replaces — and the exemption covers the whole `/dev` namespace. The assertion itself is unchanged. In plain words: the container images publish again, and the bundle ships the documentation it mounts · [BUG-0504](../bugs/BUG-0504-2026-10-13-release-images-never-publishes-bundle-check-fails.md)
- **Dashboard drops filters that belong to another server** — a target, model or tag picked on another server (or in an earlier visit) stayed selected on the Dashboard and hid every host behind "No hosts match the selected filters". The Dashboard now removes picks that are not among the current server's options, like the Device page already did. In plain words: switching server no longer leaves you looking at an empty Dashboard · [BUG-0503](../bugs/BUG-0503-2026-10-13-dashboard-keeps-filter-from-another-server.md)
- **No more "loading screen" on every page load, and the AI Agent splash stays gone** — the `AI Agent` full-screen splash was cached per **tab** (`sessionStorage`), so it came back on every new tab and every browser restart, and one failed `/server/agents/` call un-cached it for good; it is now cached per origin and recorded even when the call fails. Underneath it, every cold load was showing a **bare header and footer with an empty body** — the app withheld its navigation, server picker and workspace switcher for the whole time `AuthContext` waited on a profile round trip. The real app frame now paints immediately, and the content area is held with a neutral page skeleton (title row, four tiles, a body block — no text, no spinner, no "loading" wording) instead of nothing. `Checking authentication...` also no longer flashes just before the page arrives. In plain words: loading a page looks like a page arriving, not like a broken shell · [BUG-0502](../bugs/BUG-0502-2026-10-13-loading-screen-every-load-and-e2e-cannot-authenticate.md)
- **Product videos: no more silent voice lines** — the voice-over tool renders the voice track on its own before adding it to the video (mixing both in one ffmpeg graph dropped whole lines), writes a voice map, and fails if any line is silent; the quality gate now hears every line (`voice_silent`) and measures the decoded audio length. In plain words: a video can no longer ship with a sentence missing from its narration. [BUG-0501](../bugs/BUG-0501-2026-10-13-voiceover-drops-voice-lines.md)
- **The Tello tile can finally tell you what the drone is doing** — the status route asked the host for a page that has never existed (`/host/remote/getStatus`), so every poll 404'd. That was worse than a wrong answer: the tile aborts its whole poll on a non-2xx, so it never even reached the scan, and could only ever say "could not check" — never "AP unavailable — power on the drone", even with the drone genuinely off. The host now has a real `/host/drone/tello/status` route answering the link state, and a device with no controller answers 200 + `linked: false` rather than hiding behind another 404. In plain words: the tile reports the drone's real state instead of always failing · [BUG-0500](../bugs/BUG-0500-2026-10-13-drone-status-route-404-blocks-tile.md)
- **One unreadable alert no longer blocks the triage of all others** — when the vision model returned an empty answer for a frame, the worker paused model calls for every alert (up to an hour) and retried the same alert forever; alerts that left the 6 h window stayed untriaged. An empty or unparseable answer now only concerns that alert (skipped as `model_empty` after two tries), credit and timeout failures still pause everything, and alerts past the window are marked `expired`. Four Grafana rules got quieter: "Too many incidents on a device" no longer counts alerts the triage already settled, "Host stopped reporting" ignores the renamed `server` metrics series, "Platform service down" reads the live status table instead of the change log (it called every healthy component stale), and "Script failing repeatedly" sends one alert per device instead of one per script. In plain words: a bad frame no longer freezes alert triage, and the alert mail stops listing noise · [BUG-0499](../bugs/BUG-0499-2026-10-13-triage-blocked-by-one-empty-model-answer.md)
- **AI chat connects on its own when the key is there** — the "API key needed" popup re-checks the server every 15 s instead of staying up until a reload, the browser no longer autofills the key field, and the popup is shorter (title, one line, a settings icon, Save). In plain words: the chat no longer asks for a key it already has · [BUG-0498](../bugs/BUG-0498-2026-10-13-ai-chat-key-popup-stuck-after-one-no-key-answer.md)
- **AI usage: the Training panel loads again** — it listed samples without their id and failed with a bare `'id'`. In plain words: the training data panel shows its numbers · [BUG-0495](../bugs/BUG-0495-2026-10-12-ai-usage-training-panel-keyerror-id.md)
- **AI usage: host features are no longer counted as external API** — localize second opinion, triage and other host-side calls now show origin Monitoring. In plain words: the "API" share is only real outside callers · [BUG-0496](../bugs/BUG-0496-2026-10-12-ai-usage-host-calls-attributed-to-api.md)
- **A provider with no credit is checked for free, and background AI calls wait** — the pause now ends by reading the account's credit instead of sending a paid request that fails; Jev shadow features are skipped (and counted) while their provider is paused. In plain words: no more paying to learn there is still no credit · [BUG-0497](../bugs/BUG-0497-2026-10-12-ai-no-credit-pause-paid-probe-and-shadow-calls.md)
- **Localize crowns the screen the matcher actually chose** — on a menu the popover could gold-tint a screen the title had rejected; a tie no longer shows a winner, an abstain says why (e.g. a near-empty frame), and step 2 shows the screen state (motion, loaded, overlay, playback, error). In plain words: the popover now says the same thing the matcher decided · [BUG-0494](../bugs/BUG-0494-2026-10-12-localize-popover-crowned-ranked-not-verdict.md) · `2289edffe`
- **Tello pairing works on real hardware** — the host-side drone routes tested the `(value, error)` tuple that `require_field` returns, and a non-empty tuple is always truthy, so `/host/drone/tello/pair`, `mission_pads`, `flight_summary` and `last_known` all answered `400 {"error": ["<device_id>", null]}` before reading a single field; on top of that `'tello'` was missing from `controller_class_patterns` (so the lookup returned `None` even though the controller is built), and the AP join shelled out to `sudo nmcli`, which `vpt_user` cannot do (polkit refuses; it is not in the `network` group). All three are fixed: the routes unpack the tuple like every other host route, `tello` resolves to `TelloRemoteController`, `nmcli` is tried unprivileged before `sudo -n`, an already-associated host is a no-op rather than a failure, and the SDK link check — not the join — decides the outcome. A new `tello-wifi` sudoers drop-in grants `vpt_user` only `device wifi rescan` and `device wifi connect TELLO-*`. In plain words: pairing a real drone no longer fails with an error that names nothing · [BUG-0490](../bugs/BUG-0490-2026-10-12-tello-pair-routes-400-and-missing-controller.md)
- **Phone remote Scr↓ scrolls the page down** — the Scr buttons sent the swipe the arrow points, and a finger swiping down drags a page toward its top, so Scr↓ did nothing on a page at its top. They now send the opposite finger swipe, sized to the phone's live screen (70%→30% of its height) instead of fixed pixels. In plain words: Scr↓ on a phone, or on a group of phones, now shows what is further down the page · [BUG-0489](../bugs/BUG-0489-2026-10-12-phone-remote-scroll-buttons-scroll-the-wrong-way.md)
- **Every alert carries a frame** — a freeze with no thumbnail on disk now uploads its capture frames, and an alert whose frame did not upload is not created: it stays pending and the upload is retried every minute. In plain words: no more alerts with an empty picture that nobody (and no AI) can judge · [BUG-0493](../bugs/BUG-0493-2026-10-12-alerts-created-without-a-frame.md) · `10de3cb4c`
- **A model account with no credit is no longer called on every request** — a 402 from a model provider now pauses that endpoint for every AI usage in the process (2 min, doubling to 1 h), with one probe call per pause; alert triage no longer sends alerts without a frame to the image-only vision model, and the Alerts page shows "Triage unavailable: <reason>" while the triage model fails. In plain words: an empty OpenRouter account costs a few failed calls an hour instead of thousands, and the Alerts page tells you why alerts stay unchecked · [BUG-0492](../bugs/BUG-0492-2026-10-12-ai-no-credit-402-retried-on-every-call.md) · `fa248686b`
- **Screenshot-streamed phones follow the phone again** — a phone streamed by adb screenshots (no HDMI capture) showed every change 3-4 s late: ffmpeg decoded its PNG frames on ~20 threads, each holding a frame, and stamped them on a clock running at half speed. It now decodes on one thread and stamps frames with the real time. In plain words: in group control the screenshot phone's tile now shows a key press about as fast as the others · [BUG-0491](../bugs/BUG-0491-2026-10-12-screencap-phone-stream-lags-seconds-behind.md)
